### [66] [Beyond Homoscedasticity: Decoupled Uncertainty Optimization for Deep Imbalanced Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04995)
*Juncheng Zhou,Jiaxi Lu,Weijing Zeng,Zhong Li,Hao Qi,Jingsong Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DUO框架，用于深度不平衡回归任务。通过将回归目标建模为条件高斯分布来显式刻画实例级预测不确定性，并通过解耦均值-方差优化将不确定性转化为尾部样本的动态增强信号，同时设计分布引导的对比学习机制缓解特征松散与跨标签语义纠缠问题，在多个视觉和生物基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度不平衡回归（DIR）任务中尾部稀缺样本往往具有更高实用价值，但现有方法大多学习确定性点映射，隐式假设所有样本具有统一不确定性水平，忽视了长尾数据中广泛存在的实例级异方差性。此外，异方差负对数似然在DIR场景下存在梯度耦合问题，会削弱难尾部样本的学习信号，导致优化惰性和尾部欠拟合。

Method: DUO框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 将回归目标建模为条件高斯分布，显式表征实例级预测不确定性；(2) 通过解耦均值-方差优化，将不确定性转换为尾部样本的动态增强信号；(3) 设计分布引导的对比学习机制，基于样本分布重叠自适应构建正负样本对，缓解特征松散与跨标签语义纠缠。

Result: 在IMDB-WIKI-DIR、AgeDB-DIR和AAV2-DIR等视觉与生物基准上，DUO在few-shot bMAE和GM指标上取得最优结果，在few-shot MAE上也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: DUO通过显式建模不确定性并将之转化为尾部样本的动态学习信号，有效解决了深度不平衡回归中确定性映射忽略异方差性以及负对数似然梯度耦合导致的尾部欠拟合问题，验证了不确定性感知策略在长尾连续预测任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Deep Imbalanced Regression (DIR) is pervasive in continuous prediction tasks across diverse modalities, such as age estimation, depth prediction, and protein mutation activity prediction, where label-scarce tail samples often carry higher practical value. However, most existing methods still learn deterministic point mappings under mean squared error or its simple variants, implicitly assuming a uniform uncertainty level across all samples and thereby overlooking the instance-wise heteroscedasticity that is widespread in long-tailed data. We further point out that even heteroscedastic negative log-likelihood suffers from a gradient coupling issue, which, under DIR scenarios, weakens the learning signal of hard tail samples and leads to optimization inertia as well as tail underfitting. To address this, we propose DUO, an uncertainty-aware long-tailed regression framework. Specifically, the proposed method models the regression target as a conditional Gaussian distribution to explicitly characterize instance-level predictive uncertainty, and transforms uncertainty into a dynamic enhancement signal for tail samples through decoupled mean-variance optimization. Furthermore, we design a distribution-guided contrastive learning mechanism that adaptively constructs positive and negative pairs based on the overlap between sample distributions, thereby alleviating feature looseness and cross-label semantic entanglement. Across visual and biological DIR benchmarks, DUO achieves the best few-shot bMAE and GM on IMDB-WIKI-DIR, AgeDB-DIR, and AAV2-DIR while remaining competitive on few-shot MAE.

</details>


### [67] [Solution-space heterogeneity shapes federated learning dynamics across partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05012)
*Ping Luo,Jiahuan Wang,Ziqing Wen,Tao Sun,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出solution-space PDE-Dirichlet协议，通过将连续监督响应转换为可复用的解空间bin，并利用最优传输量化客户端间的非独立同分布(data heterogeneity)程度，为联邦偏微分方程学习提供了可转移的非IID定义。基于七个PDE任务和三种神经算子家族的实验表明，Dirichlet浓度降低会一致性地增加客户端间解距离与优化异质性，但最终误差恶化程度具有任务依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦科学机器学习允许各机构在不集中本地物理数据的情况下训练神经代理模型，但现有PDE研究缺乏关于非独立同分布数据的可转移定义。已有协议根据方程特定规则划分坐标、系数、边界条件或几何，缺乏统一且通用的非IID度量框架。

Method: 1) 提出solution-space PDE-Dirichlet协议：将连续监督响应转换为可复用的解空间bin，通过最优传输（在bin几何上）量化客户端间的实现分离程度；2) 推导种群分配异质性与Dirichlet浓度之间的精确反比关系；3) 建立响应异质性引发梯度分歧、局部更新离散和参数发散的条件；4) 在多个控制及公共PDE任务上对三种神经算子家族和五个随机种子进行系统实验验证。

Result: Dirichlet浓度越低，实现解距离与优化异质性一致性增加。最终误差退化具有任务依赖性：最显著的效果出现在低粘度Burgers方程，最异构设置下最终误差增加4.157个百分点；而额外通信或更平滑的动力学可以减少最终差距，尽管参数分离持续存在。

Conclusion: 该工作区分了可复现的几何机制与任务依赖的泛化结果，为评估联邦非IID PDE学习提供了通用基础，并揭示了响应空间异质性在联邦科学机器学习中的核心作用。

Abstract: Federated scientific machine learning enables institutions to train neural surrogates without centralizing local physical data, yet studies of partial differential equations (PDEs) lack a transferable definition of non-independent and identically distributed data. Existing protocols partition coordinates, coefficients, boundary conditions, or geometries according to equation-specific rules. Here, we introduce solution-space PDE-Dirichlet, a protocol that converts continuous supervised responses into reusable solution bins and quantifies the realized separation between clients through optimal transport over the geometry of these bins. We derive an exact inverse relation between population allocation heterogeneity and the Dirichlet concentration, and we establish conditions under which response heterogeneity induces gradient disagreement, local-update dispersion, and parameter divergence. Across seven controlled and public PDE tasks, three neural-operator families, and five random seeds, a lower concentration consistently increases the realized solution distance and optimization heterogeneity. The degradation in final error is task dependent: the largest effect occurs for low-viscosity Burgers, reaching 4.157 percentage points under the most heterogeneous setting, whereas additional communication or smoother dynamics can reduce the final gap despite persistent parameter separation. These results distinguish a reproducible geometric mechanism from task-dependent generalization outcomes and provide a common basis for evaluating non-IID federated PDE learning.

</details>


### [68] [Amortizing Scaling Law Construction Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05016)
*Abhash Kumar Jha,Diana Alexandra Onuţu,Neeratyoy Mallik,Swagatam Haldar,Sam Laing,Niccolò Ajroldi,Shiwei Liu,Joaquin Vanschoren,Aaron Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效构建缩放定律（scaling law）的框架，将数据收集问题形式化为贝叶斯优化，并通过渐进扩展计算预算和替代幻想评估（surrogate-fantasized evaluations）来恢复实验网格，从而在节省10–100倍计算量的情况下拟合出与全稠密网格相当的缩放定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推导缩放定律通常需要在超参数、token预算和参数量上训练穷举网格，计算开销极大；然而拟合缩放定律实际上只需要各计算规模下的最优损失前沿，大部分训练配置被浪费。本文致力于降低这一过程中不必要的计算浪费，使缩放定律的构建更加高效。

Method: 将缩放定律构建中的数据收集过程形式化为贝叶斯优化问题；引入在受限计算预算下对比不同缩放定律拟合方法的度量指标；采用渐进扩展获取阶段计算预算的策略（模拟实际中按计算量排序评估配置的方式）以显著提升恢复效率；利用代理模型对未观测配置进行幻想评估（surrogate-fantasized evaluations），以补全更广泛的实验网格，从而无需训练所有配置即可完成准确的缩放定律拟合。

Result: 所提出方法能够紧密匹配在全稠密网格上得到的缩放定律拟合结果，同时计算成本节省高达10–100倍。

Conclusion: 通过贝叶斯优化驱动的数据获取与代理幻想评估相结合，可以大幅降低缩放定律拟合所需的计算资源，为大规模基础模型训练的设计选择提供更高效、可扩展的指导工具。

Abstract: Scaling laws guide the design choices for training large foundation models, but deriving them involves training an exhaustive grid over hyperparameters, token budgets, and parameter counts, which is computationally expensive. Fitting a scaling law, however, only requires the best-loss frontier across compute scales, discarding most of the trained configurations. We propose a framework for efficient scaling law construction that formulates data collection as a Bayesian optimization problem, and introduce metrics for comparing scaling law fitting methods under constrained compute budgets. We find that progressively expanding the compute budget during acquisition, mirroring the compute-ordered evaluation of configurations in practice, substantially improves recovery efficiency. Augmenting the observed configurations with surrogate-fantasized evaluations then recovers the broader experimental grid, allowing accurate scaling law fitting without training every configuration. Together, these can closely match scaling law fits over a full dense grid at computational savings of up to $10\text{--}100\times$.

</details>


### [69] [Confounding-Valid Conformal Inference for Counterfactual KPIs in Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05073)
*Abdessamed Qchohi,Jessica Moysen Cortes,Matteo Zecchin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CV-CCI方法，结合丰富的观测遥测数据和有限的随机化数据，在存在隐藏混杂因子的情况下，为网络运营商提供具有有限样本覆盖保证的反事实推理预测集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络运营商需要可靠的反事实"what-if"分析，但记录遥测数据可能遗漏控制器使用的变量，导致隐藏混杂并使统计保证失效。虽可用随机化遥测解决，但随机化数据稀缺，仅依赖它会产生信息量不足的预测集。

Method: CV-CCI基于General Synthetic-Powered Inference (GESPI)原理，将丰富的、可能被混杂的观测数据与有限的随机化数据相结合：用观测数据提升预测效率，用随机化数据在任意隐藏混杂下维持有限样本覆盖保证。

Result: 在两个代表性无线接入网(RAN)控制任务上的实验表明，CV-CCI在隐藏混杂下保持有效性的同时，产生的预测集比最先进的混杂有效基线更高效。

Conclusion: CV-CCI成功解决了反事实推理中"观测数据有混杂但丰富、随机化数据无混杂但稀缺"的根本矛盾，实现了既有统计保证又高效实用的反事实推断。

Abstract: Conformal counterfactual inference enables network operators to use logged telemetry to reliably answer 'what-if' questions about network operation. These answers typically take the form of prediction sets that contain, with a user-defined probability, the key performance indicators (KPIs) that would have been observed under alternative control actions. A key challenge is that logged telemetry may omit variables used by the controller, resulting in hidden confounding and invalidating the statistical guarantees of counterfactual analysis. In principle, this issue can be addressed using randomized telemetry, collected by assigning control actions independently of the network state. However, because such randomization may disrupt normal operation, randomized telemetry is typically scarce, causing counterfactual analysis based solely on it to produce uninformative prediction sets. To address these challenges, we propose Confounding-Valid Counterfactual Conformal Inference (CV-CCI), which combines abundant, potentially confounded observational telemetry with limited randomized data through the General Synthetic-Powered Inference (GESPI) principle. CV-CCI leverages observational data to improve efficiency while using randomized data to retain finite-sample coverage guarantees under arbitrary hidden confounding. Experiments on two representative radio access network (RAN) control tasks show that CV-CCI remains valid under hidden confounding while producing more efficient prediction sets than state-of-the-art confounding-valid baselines.

</details>


### [70] [Deep Microcompression: Structured Pruning and Bit-packed Quantization for Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05081)
*Opegbemi Matthias Busoye,Tolulope Matthew Busoye,Eghonghon-aye Eigbe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出深度微压缩（DMC），一种面向裸机微控制器的硬件感知深度学习推理管线，通过结构化剪枝、量化感知训练和定长位打包实现高压缩率推理，首次在ATmega328P（2KB SRAM）上成功部署标准CNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源极度受限的裸机微控制器（尤其是SRAM仅为2KB的ATmega328P等设备）上部署标准CNN推理此前被认为不可行，现有框架如TensorFlow Lite在二进制体积和内存占用上仍难以满足这类硬件的严格约束。

Method: DMC整合了三种关键技术：结构化剪枝（移除冗余权重通道/结构）、量化感知训练（降低权重精度）、以及定长位打包（将量化后权重进行紧凑存储）。该管线生成无依赖的C库，推理延迟具有确定性，从而实现硬件感知的端到端压缩流程。

Result: 在LeNet-5上实现55.8倍权重压缩比（精度98.77%）；在RP2040（Cortex-M0+）上，二进制体积比TensorFlow Lite小3倍且精度一致；首次在仅有2KB SRAM的ATmega328P上成功部署标准CNN。

Conclusion: DMC证明了通过硬件感知的联合压缩策略（剪枝+量化+位打包），即使在2KB级SRAM的极端受限MCU上也能实现标准CNN推理，大幅拓展了微控制器上深度学习部署的可能性边界，并为该领域提供了高性能、低体积、无依赖的替代方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces Deep Microcompression (DMC), a hardware-aware pipeline for deep learning inference on bare-metal microcontrollers. DMC integrates structured pruning, quantization-aware training, and fixed-length bit-packing to achieve a 55.8$\times$ weight compression ratio on LeNet-5 (98.77\% accuracy), generating a dependency-free C library with deterministic latency. On the RP2040 (Cortex-M0+), DMC reduces binary size by 3$\times$ versus TensorFlow Lite while matching its accuracy. Critically, DMC enables the first documented deployment of a standard CNN on the ATmega328P, a device constrained to 2KB SRAM, previously considered infeasible for CNN inference.

</details>


### [71] [NEAT-POCKET: Pocket-Conditioned Autoregressive 3D Molecular Generation with a Neighborhood-Guided Set Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05097)
*Roxane Axel Jacob,Daniel Rose,Thierry Langer,Johannes Kirchmair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了NEAT-POCKET，一种以蛋白口袋为条件的自回归3D分子生成模型扩展，能在蛋白结合口袋内逐原子生成分子，同时保持原子置换不变性并显式建模氢原子。在CrossDocked和SPINDR数据集上实现了与现有基线相当的基于结构的生成性能，且采样速度显著更快，并天然支持口袋条件下的片段补全任务，适用于先导化合物优化和骨架延伸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动的de novo分子设计有望加速早期药物发现，但现有的基于结构的3D分子生成方法在采样速度和灵活性方面存在不足。作者希望开发一种既能直接在蛋白结合口袋中生成新配体，又能保持高效采样、具备置换不变性处理能力的方法，以满足药物发现中实际应用对速度和灵活性的需求。

Method: NEAT-POCKET是基于自回归NEAT模型的扩展，通过口袋条件化实现3D分子生成。模型在蛋白口袋环境中逐原子地生成分子，同时保持原子排列置换不变性，并显式建模氢原子。此外，该模型天然支持口袋条件下的片段补全任务。

Result: 在CrossDocked和SPINDR两个数据集上的基准测试表明，NEAT-POCKET在基于结构的分子生成任务中达到了与现有基线相当的性能，同时在采样速度上显著优于现有方法。此外，它成功实现了片段补全任务，验证了其在先导优化场景中的适用性。

Conclusion: NEAT-POCKET被定位为一个快速、灵活且实用的基于结构的药物设计框架，兼具全分子生成和片段补全能力，为结构基药物设计提供了一种高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: AI-driven de novo molecular design offers a promising route to accelerate early-stage drug discovery by generating novel ligands directly within target protein binding pockets. We present NEAT-POCKET, a pocket-conditioned extension of the autoregressive NEAT model for 3D molecular generation. NEAT-POCKET generates molecules atom by atom in protein pocket environments while preserving atom permutation invariance and explicitly modeling hydrogen atoms. Benchmarks on the CrossDocked and SPINDR datasets show that NEAT-POCKET achieves competitive structure-based generation performance while sampling substantially faster than existing baselines. Beyond full-molecule generation, NEAT-POCKET naturally enables pocket-conditioned fragment completion, a task directly relevant to lead optimization and scaffold elaboration. These results position NEAT-POCKET as a fast, flexible, and practical framework for structure-based drug design.

</details>


### [72] [A Comparative Study of Counterfactual Explainers for Graph Neural Networks Enabling Multiple Types of Graph Edit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05113)
*Maria Myrto Villia,Filippos Gouidis,Theodore Patkos,Panos Trahanias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对六种SOTA图反事实解释器进行了系统性比较，在多种真实与合成数据集上（覆盖二分类/多分类的图级和节点级任务）使用定量与定性指标评估其性能，旨在识别各方法的优缺点以指导未来研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图结构数据的反事实解释旨在找到使模型预测改变的最小且现实的修改，但现有支持增删边的方法缺乏通用性和高效性，且不同方法在解释规模、覆盖率和质量之间存在权衡，需要系统性比较来明确各方法的适用场景。

Method: 对六种SOTA反事实图解释模型进行对照实验，在真实与合成的多样化数据集上展开评估，覆盖二分类和多分类的图级与节点级分类任务，并结合定量指标（如解释规模、覆盖率等）与定性指标进行全面考量。

Result: 研究发现不同SOTA方法在解释的规模、覆盖率和质量之间存在明显的权衡取舍，各方法在不同任务类型和数据集上表现出各自优势和不足。

Conclusion: 通过全面比较识别出各反事实解释方法的适用边界和局限性，为图反事实解释领域的未来研究方向和技术改进提供了指引。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations for graph-structured data seek to determine minimal and realistic modifications required in an input graph to alter a model's prediction to a predefined output. Although counterfactual explainers that support modifying the graph by both adding and removing edges have recently emerged, there is still a lack of general and efficient methods, especially when considering the quality of the generated explanations. Moreover, the problem remains far from solved, as existing methods exhibit different strengths and weaknesses, often trading off between explanation size, coverage and quality. For this reason, it is important to identify where each method performs well and where it falls short, so as to guide future research in the field. Thus, our study compares six state-of-the-art (SOTA) models on a diverse set of real-world and synthetic datasets, covering both binary and multi-class graph and node classification tasks, and evaluates their performance using diverse quantitative and qualitative metrics.

</details>


### [73] [Single-Query Black-Box Calibration Auditing via Logit Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05125)
*Roman Plaud,Antoine Saillenfest,Matthieu Labeau,Thomas Bonald,Willem Waegeman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在商业LLM API隐藏连续输出概率的情况下，利用logit_bias参数通过单次查询精确评估概率阈值的方法，并引入了一个新的、可证明一致的二分类任务True Calibration Error估计器，为黑盒基础模型的校准审计提供了高效框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）作为零样本分类器的安全部署依赖于校准评估，但商业API提供商日益隐藏校准指标所需的连续输出概率（logits），导致标准校准度量无法直接计算，亟需一种在信息不透明条件下仍能有效评估模型校准性的方法。

Method: 作者证明任何暴露logit_bias参数的LLM API都可以在数学上被操控，仅需每个样本一次查询即可精确评估概率阈值。基于这一机制，提出了一种新的、可证明一致的二分类任务True Calibration Error（TCE）估计器，用于审计黑盒基础模型。

Result: 该方法提供了单查询级别的精确概率阈值评估能力，并证明所提出的TCE估计器具有一致性，为黑盒LLM的校准审计提供了高效且理论上有保障的工具。

Conclusion: 本文通过利用logit_bias机制的数学特性，克服了商业API隐藏概率输出带来的校准评估障碍，为黑盒基础模型的安全部署审计提供了一条可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Evaluating the calibration of Large Language Models (LLMs) is critical for their safe deployment as zero-shot classifiers. Yet, commercial API providers increasingly hide the continuous output probabilities required by standard calibration metrics. To bypass this opacity, we demonstrate that any LLM API exposing a logit\_bias parameter can be mathematically manipulated to evaluate exact probability thresholds using strictly one query per sample. Leveraging this mechanism, we introduce a novel and provably consistent estimator of the True Calibration Error for binary tasks. Our approach therefore provides an efficient framework for auditing black-box foundation models.

</details>


### [74] [Coarse-Graining Hidden Representations: Unsupervised Neuron Selection via Mapping Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05126)
*Margherita Mele,Andrea Castagna,Roberto Menichetti,Raffaello Potestio,Alessandro Ingrosso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于映射熵（Mapping Entropy, ME）的完全无监督神经元选择准则，用于对过参数化神经网络的隐藏层进行粗粒化，选出最有信息量的神经子集。实验表明在教师-学生网络、非线性高斯过程和MNIST等任务上，ME选择的子网络优于同等规模的随机子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化神经网络往往包含远超任务所需的隐藏单元，因此需要回答哪些神经元是关键的，以及这种区分是否能在无标签、无梯度的条件下从表示本身读取。已有方法依赖监督信号或梯度，本文希望建立一种仅基于隐藏层激活统计的无监督选择标准。

Method: 将神经元选择形式化为隐藏层的粗粒化问题，即保留神经元的子集；用映射熵（ME）量化丢弃部分神经元所导致的判别力损失，以最小化ME的神经元子集作为信息量最大的选择。该准则仅依赖隐藏激活统计量，完全无监督。

Result: 在教师-学生网络中，ME优化可恢复最小教师一致的表示，并按隐藏层残余变异的比例保留额外单元；在非线性高斯过程任务中，ME选择出功能一致的类映射，其偏好类别随训练过程漂移。在非线性高斯过程任务和翻译增强MNIST上，ME选出的子网络在强压缩下明显优于同等规模的随机子集。

Conclusion: ME为无监督场景下识别网络中关键神经元提供了一种可行且有效的准则，并将表示的构型可区分性与预测性能联系起来，表明神经元的可判别性与其对下游任务的价值高度相关。

Abstract: Overparameterized neural networks carry far more hidden units than a task nominally requires, raising the question of which neurons are essential and whether that distinction is legible in the representation itself, without labels or gradients. We cast neuron selection as the problem of coarse-graining the hidden layer by retaining a subset of its neurons, and score each putative selection by the mapping entropy (ME). This quantity measures the loss of discriminatory power inherent in discarding part of the network neurons, and the selection that minimises the ME is taken as particularly informative. This criterion is fully unsupervised, in that it depends only on hidden-activation statistics. In teacher-student networks, ME optimisation recovers the minimal teacher-consistent representation and retains extra units in proportion to the hidden layer's residual variability; in a non-linear Gaussian process task, it selects coherent functional-class mappings whose preferred class shifts across training. On this task and on translation-augmented MNIST, ME-selected subnetworks outperform random subsets of equal size, most clearly under strong compression - linking configurational distinguishability to predictive performance.

</details>


### [75] [MomentQuant: an even more minimalist interval method with linear time complexity for time series classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05136)
*Johann Faouzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MomentQuant算法，通过优化Quant的实现并使用Cornish-Fisher展开推导近似分位数（替代精确分位数），显著提升时间序列分类算法的推理速度，仅以微小的预测性能下降为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列分类算法在实际应用中需要在预测性能与计算成本之间权衡。Quant算法虽然能在区间提取分位数实现高精度且速度较快，但在实际应用中推理（inference）比训练更频繁，因此进一步提升推理速度对真实应用具有重要意义。

Method: 提出两项改进：1）对Quant算法进行更优化的代码实现，保持算法不变但提升执行效率；2）提出MomentQuant，使用Cornish-Fisher展开计算近似分位数替代精确分位数，从而避免对时间序列进行排序，降低计算复杂度。

Result: 实验证据表明：优化后的Quant实现比原始Quant更快；MomentQuant比优化版Quant更快，仅以极小的预测性能损失为代价。这些性能改进特别适合推理频率远高于训练频率的实际应用场景。

Conclusion: 通过算法优化和近似分位数策略，可以大幅提升时间序列分类的推理速度，在保持高精度的同时满足实际应用中对响应速度的需求。

Abstract: Time series data is very common in many real-world applications and in numerous domains, with increasing interest for automated information extraction using machine learning. One of these subfields is time series classification, which consists in assigning a label to each new, unseen time series. Many algorithms have been developed over the past decades, with the trade-off between predictive performance and computational cost being consistently discussed. Quant, an interval-based algorithm extracting quantiles from recursive, fixed, dyadic intervals, was shown to achieve high accuracy, while being very fast. We propose two changes to make this algorithm even faster. The first one is a better optimized implementation of the exact same algorithm. The second one is to derive approximate quantiles, using the Cornish-Fisher expansion, instead of exact quantiles. This change removes the necessity to sort the time series, leading to a smaller computational complexity. We call this novel algorithm MomentQuant. We provide evidence that our implementation of Quant is faster than the original one, and that MomentQuant is even faster than our implementation of Quant, at the cost of a tiny decrease in predictive performance. These improvements are especially relevant for real-life applications, where inference is performed much more often than training.

</details>


### [76] [From 80x to 385x: A Best-Matching-Unit Search at the L2 Roof, Measured Against a Symmetrically Tuned Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05138)
*Andrew James Amos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 作者对称地调优了自己提出的SOM算法(SparseBin)和基线算法(cuSPARSE)，通过四个优化杠杆将epoch性能提升5.6-10.1倍，并将与CUDA实现的差距从~80x扩大到~385x。cuSPARSE同样被调优后提速2-3倍。调优后的核函数达到L2带宽峰值的77%，其他单元为40-65%，表明这是一个终止性结果而非中间路标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU实现之间的比较通常不对称——一方由作者精心调试，另一方则直接运行未调优的默认配置。本文旨在纠正这种不公平比较，对基线算法(cuSPARSE)给予与自有算法(SparseBin)同等程度的调优关注。

Method: 通过四个优化杠杆调试自组织映射训练中占主导地位的最近匹配单元(BMU)搜索：(1) tile size（瓦片大小）、(2) tile-membership clustering（瓦片成员聚类）、(3) neuron-axis chunking（神经元轴分块）、(4) vectorised loads（向量化加载）。同时对基线算法cuSPARSE应用其侧可对应的每种杠杆进行对称优化。

Result: 调优后SparseBin在每个epoch上较此前已发表配置提升5.6-10.1倍（map尺寸32x32至512x512），对底层CUDA实现的性能优势从~80倍提升至~385倍。对称调优后的cuSPARSE也提速2-3倍。调优后的核函数达到L2带宽峰值77%，其余单元为40-65%，上限约1.3倍。

Conclusion: 该调优结果是终止性的而非中间路标——所有未测试的杠杆要么被L2带宽瓶颈(77%峰值)构建性地限制在~1.3x上限内，要么被验证为无效(null)，表明该方法已基本收敛到硬件带宽极限。

Abstract: Comparisons between GPU implementations are usually asymmetric: one side is tuned by its author, the other is run as found. I report a programme that tuned both a novel SOM algorithm (SparseBin) and the baseline algorithm it was being compared to (cuSPARSE). The best-matching-unit search that dominates self-organizing map training was tuned through four levers - tile size, tile-membership clustering, neuron-axis chunking and vectorised loads - reaching 5.6-10.1x per epoch over the previously published configuration at map sizes from 32x32 to 512x512, and lifting the margin over the CUDA implementation behind our earlier MEDLINE atlases from ~80x to ~385x. cuSPARSE, the implementation SparseBin is compared against, received every lever with an analogue on its side, and became 2-3x faster in the process. The tuned kernel pressed the L2 bandwidth roof at 77% of peak with every other unit at 40-65%, bounding any further lever at ~1.3x - a terminal result rather than a waypoint, and every untested lever was either capped by that bound by construction or measured null.

</details>


### [77] [Beyond Stationarity in Time Series: Discovering Causal Structures and Latent Regimes via Markov Blankets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05150)
*Lei Zan,Charles K. Assaad,Emilie Devijver,Eric Gaussier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RCBNB-MB算法，一种面向时间序列的因果发现方法，它放松了单一、时间一致的因果结构这一常见假设，通过识别潜在因果体制（regime）并在每个体制内发现因果图，结合马尔可夫毯以增强鲁棒性。论文提供理论保障，并在模拟数据和真实IT监控数据上验证了其优于基线方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的时间序列通常表现出体制变化（regime changes），这与传统因果发现方法假设的静态因果结构相矛盾。现有方法难以处理非平稳时间序列中因果结构随时间变化的场景，需要一种能够识别不同因果体制并在每个体制内进行因果发现的算法。

Method: RCBNB-MB采用迭代策略：首先将时间序列分割为多个因果体制（每个体制内因果结构稳定），然后在每个体制内进行因果图发现。算法利用马尔可夫毯（Markov blanket）而非直接的父节点集合，以提高对因果发现误差的鲁棒性并保留预测信息。论文提供了算法能够恢复体制转换点和因果图的理论保证。

Result: 在已知ground truth的模拟数据和真实IT监控数据上的大量实验表明，RCBNB-MB在准确检测体制变化及其关联因果图方面系统性优于基线方法，验证了其作为非平稳时间序列分析框架的鲁棒性和通用性。

Conclusion: RCBNB-MB为处理非平稳时间序列中的因果发现提供了一种鲁棒且灵活的框架，通过结合体制感知分割与基于约束和噪声的因果发现，并利用马尔可夫毯增强抗误差能力，在理论与实证层面均展现了有效性。

Abstract: This paper introduces Regime-aware Constraint-Based and Noise-Based causal discovery with Markov Blankets (RCBNB-MB), a novel causal discovery algorithm for time series that relaxes the common assumption of a single, time-consistent causal structure. Time series are typically observed at discrete time points and often exhibit regime changes that challenge the assumption of a static causal structure, a limitation in many real-world dynamic systems. To address this challenge, RCBNB-MB identifies latent causal regimes, defined as subsets of time points within which a stable causal structure holds. The algorithm follows an iterative strategy that segments the time series into regimes and discovers the causal graph within each regime. By leveraging the Markov blanket rather than direct parents, RCBNB-MB gains robustness to errors in causal discovery and preserves predictive information. We provide theoretical guarantees for RCBNB-MB's ability to recover both regime transitions and causal graphs under reasonable assumptions. Furthermore, we validate its effectiveness through extensive experiments on simulated datasets with known ground truth and real-world IT monitoring data, where taking into account regime shifts is critical. Empirical results show that RCBNB-MB systematically outperforms baseline approaches in accurately detecting regime changes and their associated causal graphs, positioning it as a robust and versatile framework for non-stationary time series analysis.

</details>


### [78] [Phase Transition Frequency as a Training Time Predictor of Test Accuracy in ResNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05194)
*Arunan J*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了ResNet微调过程中离散类别可分性跳变次数（transition count）作为最终测试准确率的预测指标。在75个实验（涵盖4个基准数据集、3种架构）中发现，该指标在标准i.i.d.分类任务上与最终准确率呈强负相关（CIFAR-10上r=-0.84，CIFAR-100上r=-0.87），但在分布偏移条件下相关性减弱（TinyImageNet r=-0.45，CIFAR-10-C r=-0.19）。部分相关性分析控制架构深度后，CIFAR-100上仍具显著预测力（r_partial=-0.69, p=0.007）。对比六种替代训练曲线信号，transition count在CIFAR上表现最佳或接近最佳，但在两个压力基准上被其他信号超越。该指标被定位为一种分布内训练质量探针，并提供了低成本检测流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找一种在训练过程中即可低成本预测最终测试准确率的信号，以避免昂贵的完整训练运行。该指标是离散类别可分性跳变（class-separability transition count）——训练过程中损失函数不同阶段间类别可分性的离散跳跃次数，作者希望检验其作为训练质量预测指标的效力。

Method: 作者对ResNet微调过程中类别可分性的离散跳变次数进行计数，并通过75个系统性实验（CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet、CIFAR-10-C基准，覆盖ResNet-18/50/101架构，每配置5-10个种子）实证检验该指标与最终测试准确率的相关性。辅助分析包括：控制架构深度（连续协变量）的部分相关性分析；与六种替代训练曲线信号的预测力比较。

Result: 在标准i.i.d.基准上获得强负相关：CIFAR-10为r=-0.84（p<10^-8, n=30），CIFAR-100为r=-0.87（p<10^-5, n=15）。分布压力下相关性减弱：TinyImageNet r=-0.45，CIFAR-10-C r=-0.19。控制架构深度后CIFAR-100部分相关性r_par=-0.69（p=0.007），但更严格的分类别条件化在n=15下未确立。与六种替代信号比较，transition count在CIFAR-100上最强、CIFAR-10上接近最强，但在两个压力基准上被超越。

Conclusion: transition count可作为分布内（in-distribution）训练质量探针，尤其适用于标准i.i.d.场景；其预测力在分布偏移下衰减。作者明确界定适用边界：与有效秩、Hessian尖锐度、Fisher信息、margin及神经坍缩度量等文献中最强竞争指标的对比不在本研究范围内，留作开放问题。文章提供了一套廉价检测流程，便于集成到标准训练循环中作为日志记录。

Abstract: The number of discrete class-separability jumps observed during ResNet finetuning is examined empirically as a predictor of final test accuracy. Across 75 experiments spanning four benchmarks (CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, and CIFAR-10-C) and three architectures (ResNet-18, ResNet-50, and ResNet-101), with five to ten seeds per configuration, a strong within-dataset negative correlation is obtained on standard i.i.d. classification benchmarks: \(r = -0.84\) on CIFAR-10 (\(p < 10^{-8}\), \(n = 30\)) and \(r = -0.87\) on CIFAR-100 (\(p < 10^{-5}\), \(n = 15\)). Under distributional stress, the relationship attenuates: TinyImageNet yields \(r = -0.45\), and the CIFAR-10-C corruption benchmark yields \(r = -0.19\). Two additional analyses discipline the empirical claim. A partial correlation controlling for architecture depth, treated as a linear covariate, shows that on CIFAR-100 the transition count retains statistically significant predictive power (\(r_{\mathrm{partial}} = -0.69\), \(p = 0.007\)); the corresponding result under the stricter categorical conditioning is not established at \(n = 15\). A comparison against six alternative training-curve signals shows that transition count achieved the strongest correlation among the evaluated signals on CIFAR-100 and one of the strongest on CIFAR-10, but is dominated by other signals on the two stressed benchmarks. The comparison is restricted to training-curve-level signals; comparisons against effective rank, Hessian sharpness, Fisher information, margin, and neural-collapse measures, which are the strongest competitors in the current literature, are not part of the present study and remain open. The observation is presented as an in-distribution training-quality probe among a family of candidate probes, and an inexpensive detection procedure suitable for logging alongside a standard training loop is provided.

</details>


### [79] [Dimension-Adaptive Batched Lipschitz Narrowing Without Knowing the Zooming Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05214)
*Yasong Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Count-Adaptive BLiN算法，消除了原A-BLiN算法中对缩放维度d_z的依赖。该算法通过从前一阶段幸存的立方体数量中选择下一阶段边长，不再使用d_z和缩放常数C_z，仍能达到Õ_d(T^{(d_z+1)/(d_z+2)})遗憾界和O_d(log log T)批次复杂度，当d_z未知时批次复杂度仍保持最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原A-BLiN算法的边长选择（ACE序列）依赖于缩放维度d_z这一先验知识，而在实际应用中d_z可能是未知的，因此需要消除该依赖。

Method: 提出Count-Adaptive策略：从前一阶段淘汰后幸存的立方体数量（即count）来引导下一阶段的边长选择，从而无需d_z或C_z即可自适应地构造边长三角。

Result: 该算法在不需要d_z和C_z的前提下达到Õ_d(T^{(d_z+1)/(d_z+2)})遗憾界以及O_d(log log T)批次复杂度；结合原文定理10的自适应网格下界，d_z未知时批次复杂度的最优阶仍为Θ_d(log log T)。

Conclusion: 通过Counting策略消除了对缩放维度d_z的依赖，使得算法在维度和相关常数未知时仍保持最优批次复杂度，理论结果与下界匹配。

Abstract: The Appropriately Combined Edge-length (ACE) sequence in A-BLiN depends on the zooming dimension $d_z$. This note removes that dependence. The next edge length is selected from the number of cubes that survive the preceding elimination. The resulting Count-Adaptive BLiN algorithm does not use $d_z$ or the zooming constant $C_z$, yet it attains $\widetilde{\mathcal O}_d(T^{(d_z+1)/(d_z+2)})$ regret with $\mathcal O_d(\log\log T)$ batches. Together with the adaptive-grid lower bound in Theorem 10 of the original paper, the optimal batch complexity remains $Θ_d(\log\log T)$ when $d_z$ is unknown.

</details>


### [80] [FedDRAW: Federated Dual Reputation Annealing Weighting for Heterogeneous Multi-Institutional Chest Radiograph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05223)
*Maryam Moradpour,Anne-Christin Hauschild*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedDRAW（联邦双声誉退火加权），一种服务器端聚合方法，通过结合数据量先验与客户端-全局参数的余弦相似度，在双退火调度下动态调整联邦学习中的客户端聚合权重，解决FedAvg中按样本量加权导致的小但信息量高的医院被弱化、大客户端可能主导全局模型的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习允许医院在不集中患者数据的情况下共同训练诊断模型，但传统的FedAvg聚合权重与本地样本数成正比，导致样本量小的医院影响力被永久压低，而样本量大的客户端即使信息量较低也可能主导全局模型，造成模型的偏差。

Method: FedDRAW采用服务器端聚合方法：将数据量先验与客户端参数和全局参数之间的余弦相似度相结合，通过两个耦合的退火调度来控制——内层调度将客户端声誉从数据量先验逐渐过渡到相似度；外层延迟的退火调度作用于softmax逆温度，在早期和中期训练轮次保持权重的选择性，并在收敛时将其放宽至均匀分布。

Result: 在CheXpert和ChestMNIST两个胸部X光数据集上，模拟了12种客户端划分场景，与7种联邦基线方法在相同本地训练设置下对比。FedDRAW在AUC和灵敏度/特异性的几何均数（GM）两个指标上取得了所有8种方法中最高的平均排名，Friedman检验及Nemenyi事后分析证实方法间差异具有统计学显著性。

Conclusion: 与仅按样本数固定权重相比，调度两个信息信号（数据量先验与参数相似度）能够减少诊断模型的偏差，为联邦学习中训练更公平、更准确的医学诊断模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence models are promising for medical diagnosis, but they require large numbers of unbiased data, which in medicine are distributed across hospitals and cannot be centralized to protect patient privacy. Federated Learning (FL) addresses this, since hospitals train one shared diagnostic model while patient data remain local. Training proceeds in communication rounds, in which each hospital trains the shared model locally and returns it to the server for merging by weighted average. This aggregation weight determines whose institutional knowledge shapes the result. Federated averaging (FedAvg) sets it in proportion to local sample count, so a small but informative hospital is permanently assigned a small influence, andl argest clients could dominate the global model even when they are less informative. We propose Federated Dual Reputation Annealing Weighting (FedDRAW), a server-side aggregation method that combines a data-size prior with the cosine similarity between client and global parameters under two coupled annealing schedules. An inner schedule shifts client reputation from the size prior towards similarity. An outer, deferred annealing schedule on the softmax inverse temperature keeps the weighting selective in the early and middle rounds and relaxes it to uniformity at convergence. We evaluate FedDRAW on 12 simulated client-partition scenarios of two chest radiograph datasets (CheXpert and ChestMNIST), against seven federated baselines under identical local training settings. FedDRAW achieved the highest average rank among all eight methods under both AUC and the geometric mean (GM) of sensitivity and specificity, which a Friedman test with Nemenyi post-hoc analysis confirmed to be a statistically significant difference between the methods. Scheduling two signals, rather than fixing the weights by sample count alone, could enable less biased diagnostic models.

</details>


### [81] [Hessian-based molecular conformation augmentation for a scalable and efficient strategy of machine learning interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05233)
*Bumju Kwak,Jeonghee Jo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两种基于Hessian导出的数据增强方法（UniAug和ModeAug），通过泰勒展开生成位移样本，在不修改训练目标和扩展autograd图的前提下，将Hessian信息间接融入机器学习原子间势的训练，提升模型精度并保持即插即用特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MLIP通常仅用能量和力训练，忽略了Hessian信息，但Hessian对振动分析和过渡态搜索等实际应用至关重要；现有显式将Hessian纳入训练目标的方法需要架构修改且因高阶反向传播带来显著计算与内存开销。

Method: 提出两种数据增强方案：(1) UniAug——各向同性高斯位移，基于简单泰勒展开生成能量和力的位移样本；(2) ModeAug——按正则模式加权位移，利用简正模式信息进行更有物理意义的扰动。两种方法均不改变训练损失函数，也不扩展反传图，可无缝集成于现有架构和训练流程。

Result: 在非平衡和平衡数据集上的综合评估表明，所提方法能提升模型精度，并提供面向具体任务（如振动分析或过渡态搜索）的实用指导原则。

Conclusion: 基于Hessian的数据增强是一种高效、轻量化利用Hessian信息的途径，兼具模型精度提升和即插即用灵活性，为MLIP在实际应用中的Hessian相关任务提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: While machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have successfully learned potential energy surfaces (PES) and atomic forces, many practical applications, such as vibrational analysis and transition state search, rely heavily on the PES Hessian. Yet, standard MLIPs tend to be trained on energy and forces alone, leaving Hessian information largely unexploited. Meanwhile, existing methods that explicitly incorporate the Hessian into training objectives require architectural modifications and introduce significant computational and memory overheads due to higher-order backpropagation. To address these limitations, we propose two Hessian-derived data augmentation schemes: isotropic Gaussian displacement (\textbf{UniAug}) and normal mode-weighted displacement (\textbf{ModeAug}). Both methods utilize simple Taylor expansions, achieving effective augmentation without altering training objectives or extending the autograd graph. This allows seamless, plug-and-play integration with existing architectures and training pipelines. Comprehensive evaluations across non-equilibrium and equilibrium datasets demonstrate that our approach enhances model accuracy while providing practical, task-specific guidelines.

</details>


### [82] [GLASS: Graph-Language Alignment with Spherical Scoring for Transferable Graph-Level Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05253)
*Xudong Wang,Chris Ding,Tongxin Li,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GLASS框架，通过图-语言在单位超球面上的对齐实现图级异常检测（GLAD）的跨域迁移。利用GraphDP将图的局部、全局和语义属性序列化为文本桥，并通过球形多模态评分（SMS）将异常检测建模为超球面上的密度估计，支持零样本和少样本跨域异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图级异常检测任务中，不同领域之间存在显著的分布差异和语义鸿沟，现有GLAD方法难以有效地将模型从一个图域迁移到另一个图域，缺乏领域无关的统一表示空间与异常评分机制。

Method: GLASS包含三个核心组件：(1) 图-语言对齐：通过在单位超球面上用多切片软余弦目标对齐结构感知图编码器与指令感知文本嵌入，构建统一表示空间；(2) GraphDP：将图的局部、全局和语义属性序列化为紧凑的Graph Descriptor Prompt作为文本桥，并利用Matryoshka表示切片强制多尺度一致；(3) SMS评分：将异常检测形式化为对齐超球面上的密度估计，在图和文本嵌入空间中实例化von Mises-Fisher核密度估计器，实现结构与语义异常信号的融合，并恢复角k近邻评分为高浓度极限情形。零样本通过在无目标域训练数据情况下编码目标域GraphDP实现，少样本通过参考集校准实现。

Result: 在十二个基准数据集和三个元域上，GLASS相较于最新的先进GLAD基线取得了最佳的平均AUROC和排名，并实现了有效的跨域迁移。

Conclusion: GLASS通过图语言对齐构建的统一超球面表示空间有效解决了跨域图级异常检测的可迁移性问题；基于密度估计的球形多模态评分提供了理论上有原则的异常信号融合方式；GraphDP文本桥机制使得零样本与少样本跨域检测成为可能。

Abstract: We introduce GLASS, a framework for graph-level anomaly detection (GLAD) that achieves robust cross-domain transferability through graph-language alignment on the unit hypersphere. GLASS builds a unified representation space by aligning a structure-aware graph encoder with an instruction-aware text embedding via a multi-slice soft cosine objective. Our framework serializes local, global, and semantic graph properties into a compact Graph Descriptor Prompt (GraphDP), creating a text bridge that enables domain-agnostic anomaly scoring. By enforcing multi-scale consistency through Matryoshka representation slices, the model captures anomalous deviations at multiple levels of granularity. For scoring, we formulate anomaly detection as density estimation on the aligned hypersphere and introduce Spherical Multi-Modal Scoring (SMS), which instantiates von Mises-Fisher kernel density estimators in both graph and text embedding spaces. This probabilistic formulation recovers angular k-nearest-neighbor scoring as a high-concentration limiting case and provides a principled fusion of structural and semantic anomaly signals. The shared text embedding space further serves as a cross-domain bridge: by encoding a target domain's GraphDP without target-domain training data, GLASS performs zero-shot anomaly detection, and with only a handful of normal examples, few-shot adaptation via reference-set calibration. Across twelve benchmarks and three meta-domains, GLASS obtains the best average AUROC and rank compared with recent advanced GLAD baselines and enables effective cross-domain transfer.

</details>


### [83] [How to Speculate about Uncertainty in Agentic Coding? A Draft-Model Gate Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05274)
*Konstantin Grotov,Valentin Malykh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出推测不确定性（Speculative Uncertainty, SU）方法，仅利用黑盒LLM agent的输出token（无需logits、权重、激活或重复采样）即可恢复预测性失败信号，通过在单次前向传播中用小型开源草稿模型对agent已生成的轨迹进行打分，提取基于推理与行为片段分离的相位感知特征并校准，为下游策略提供失败可能性分数作为事前执行否决门控，在软件工程agent上降低6-8%执行错误率和14-19%的token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent在软件工程部署中会高成本地失败——它们表现出自信的错误行为，但糟糕的决策只有在昂贵执行和重试之后才被识别。现有不确定性估计方法通常需要访问logits、权重或多次采样，而实际部署的黑盒agent难以满足这些条件，因此需要一种通用的、可操作的失败预测信号。

Method: 核心方法为"推测解码的反转"：使用小型开源权重草稿模型对已生成的agent轨迹进行评分（单次前向传播），注意模型反转了推测解码的方向——不是用草稿模型预测目标模型，而是用草稿模型评估目标模型已生成的输出。从这些推测性交叉似然中提取相位感知特征，将推理片段与行为片段分开处理，并针对可验证目标进行校准。最终产生一个失败似然分数，可被路由、人工干预或额外测试时算力等下游策略直接使用。

Result: 在软件工程agent（Qwen3-Coder-480B和闭源的Claude 3.5 Sonnet）上实例化事前执行否决门控策略，执行错误率降低6-8个百分点，token成本降低14-19%；可迁移到分布外基准而无需重新训练，并能跨不同agent模型泛化。

Conclusion: SU方法证明了仅从黑盒agent的输出token即可提取可靠的失败预测信号，为agent系统的部署提供了一种实用且可操作的安全机制，在不牺牲能力的前提下通过事前干预显著降低执行失败与资源浪费，具有跨模型和跨分布的泛化能力。

Abstract: LLM agents deployed for software engineering fail expensively: they act confidently wrong, and bad actions are recognized only after costly execution and retry. We present Speculative Uncertainty (SU), a method that recovers a predictive failure signal for a black-box agent from its output tokens alone, with no access to logits, weights, activations, or repeated sampling. Inverting speculative decoding, a small open-weight draft model scores the agent's already-generated trajectory in a single forward pass. From these speculative cross-likelihoods we extract phase-aware features by separating the reasoning and action spans, and calibrate them against a verifiable objective. SU produces a failure-likelihood score that any downstream policy, such as routing, human intervention, or extra test-time compute, can consume directly. To show the signal is actionable, we instantiate one such policy, a pre-execution veto gate, on software engineering agents Qwen3-Coder-480B and closed-source Claude 3.5 Sonnet, cutting execution error rate by 6-8 percentage points and token cost by 14-19% in deployment, transferring to out-of-distribution benchmarks without retraining, and generalizing across agent models.

</details>


### [84] [Learning from VAE Errors to support ECG-based Differential Diagnosis of Myocardial Scar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05294)
*Shayan Sharifi,Riccardo Treu,Ilaria Gandin,Federico Garoia,Marco Merlo,Giulia Cisotto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在300名心肌病患者的本地队列中，评估了β-VAE衍生的ECG表征能否区分LGE+（心肌瘢痕阳性）与LGE-患者。基础模型ECGx.AI在随机森林下达到AUC 0.686，而提出的β-VAE仅达0.577；但DTW重建误差在12个导联中的10个上显示出显著的组间差异，结合逻辑回归可达AUC 0.643，提示其可作为瘢痕相关ECG改变的潜在标志物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LGE心脏磁共振成像是心肌瘢痕的关键标志，但其可及性有限，因此需要基于常规ECG的筛查方法来进行替代或预筛。

Method: 在300名受试者的本地队列中，对比了来自基础模型ECGx.AI的32维特征与在正常PTB-XL ECG上训练的β-VAE特征。采用随机森林和梯度提升进行下游分类，并基于动态时间规整（DTW）计算重建误差，用Mann-Whitney U检验评估其组间差异。

Result: ECGx.AI结合随机森林达到AUC 0.686；β-VAE结合梯度提升达AUC 0.577（灵敏度0.775）；DTW重建误差在12个导联中10个导联上呈现显著组间差异，结合逻辑回归达到AUC 0.643。

Conclusion: DTW重建误差有望作为心肌瘢痕相关ECG改变的标志物，辅助LGE+患者的ECG筛查，尽管其分类性能仍不及基础模型ECGx.AI，但比纯β-VAE特征更具判别潜力。

Abstract: Late Gadolinium Enhancement (LGE) on cardiac magnetic resonance is a key marker of myocardial scar, but its limited accessibility motivates routine ECG-based screening. We evaluated whether $β$-variational autoencoder (VAE)-derived ECG representations can discriminate LGE+ from LGE- cardiomyopathic patients in a local cohort of 300 subjects. We compared 32-dimensional features from the foundation ECGx.AI model with those from a shallower $β$-VAE trained on normal PTB-XL ECGs, evaluating downstream classification and Dynamic Time Warping (DTW)-based reconstruction errors. ECGx.AI reached an area under ROC of 0.686 with Random Forest, while the proposed $β$-VAE reached 0.577 with sensitivity of 0.775 with Gradient Boosting. Notably, DTW-reconstruction errors significantly differed between classes in 10 out of 12 leads according to Mann-Whitney U test and help in classification, leading to an area under ROC of 0.643 with Logistic Regression, supporting their potential as markers of scar-related ECG alterations.

</details>


### [85] [How Does mHC Use Its Residual Streams? Selective Routing and Near-Identity Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05309)
*Pengxiang Zhao,Xing Li,Xianzhi Yu,Wei Guo,Zhenhua Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过有效流计数、跨流残差权重和流间余弦相似度等指标，系统分析了DeepSeek-V4-Flash四流残差通路中Hyper-Connections（mHC）的实际使用模式。研究发现：块的读/写路由集中但随深度变化（典型位置约用两个流），残差混合主要发生在浅层（22-42层基本独立传输），流间表征保持方向性区分。干预实验表明，后期混合器替换为恒等函数仅使困惑度增加1.9%，而早期混合器替换则增加41%，说明模型仅利用了mHC灵活性的一部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hyper-Connections及其流形约束变体mHC将残差通路从单流扩展为多流（n流），但训练后的模型究竟如何使用这种额外容量尚不明确。具体而言，作者希望回答三个问题：块在多大范围上进行读写、残差通路混合流的强度如何、各流是否携带不同的表征。

Method: 使用三个分析指标：有效流计数（衡量块实际读写的流数量）、跨流残差权重（衡量残差通路中流混合程度）、流间余弦相似度（衡量各流表征的区分度）。在此基础上设计针对性的干预实验：替换早期/后期混合器为恒等函数、固定混合器为其C4诊断均值、保留每token最大的三个路由权重，分别测量C4困惑度和六任务平均分值的变化。

Result: (1) 读/写路由集中但随深度变化，典型注意力或FFN位置实际约使用两个流，主导流随层变化；(2) 残差混合适度且主要发生在浅层，第22-42层中残差通路大体将各流分开传输；(3) 流间表征保持方向性区分；(4) 替换后期混合器为恒等函数仅使C4困惑度增加1.9%且六任务平均分不变，替换早期混合器则困惑度增加41%；(5) 固定早期混合器至C4均值和保留三个最大路由权重分别只造成很小的性能损失。

Conclusion: DeepSeek-V4-Flash仅实现了四流mHC所提供的灵活性的一部分：单个块很少需要全部四个流，后期残差混合带来的可测收益很小。模型的实际使用模式呈现"浅层混合、深层分离、每块约两流"的高效结构，表明训练后的模型在多流架构中自然收敛到一种集约化的路由模式。

Abstract: Hyper-Connections and their manifold-constrained variant mHC widen a residual pathway from one stream to n, yet how trained models use this capacity remains unclear: how broadly blocks read and write, how strongly the residual pathway mixes streams, and whether the streams carry distinct representations. We examine these properties in the four-stream residual pathway of DeepSeek-V4-Flash using effective stream counts, cross-stream residual weights, and inter-stream cosine similarity. Read/write routing is concentrated but varies across depth: a typical attention or FFN site effectively uses about two streams, while the dominant stream changes across layers and the representations remain directionally distinct. Residual mixing is modest and occurs primarily in early layers; in layers 22-42, the pathway mostly carries each stream forward separately. Targeted interventions establish the functional significance of these patterns. Replacing the late mixers by identity increases C4 perplexity by only 1.9% and preserves the six-task average score, whereas replacing the early mixers increases perplexity by 41%. Fixing each early mixer to its C4 diagnostic mean increases perplexity by only 0.2% and reduces the average score by 0.25 percentage points, showing that its site-specific structure matters more than its token-wise variation on the evaluated metrics. Likewise, retaining the three largest routing weights per token at every site increases perplexity by at most 2.7% and changes the average score by at most 0.4 points. Thus, the studied model realizes only part of the flexibility afforded by four-stream mHC: individual blocks rarely require all four streams, and late residual mixing provides little measured benefit.

</details>


### [86] [Optimal Rates for Agentic Networked Information Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05318)
*MohammadHossein Bateni,Zahra Hadizadeh,MohammadTaghi Hajiaghayi,Mahdi JafariRaviz,Shayan Taherijam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了网络化学习模型中的信息聚合问题，关闭了Kearns等人之前留下的渐近间隙：证明了在线性回归和逻辑回归（logistic分类）两种设定下，深度为D的M覆盖路径上最后一个智能体的超额误差的精确速率为：在深度达到M²之前为常数，之后为Θ(M²/D)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kearns, Roth和Ryu (SODA'26)提出了网络化学习模型并给出了超额MSE的上界O(M/√D)和循环实例的下界Ω(M/D)，二者之间存在间隙。此外他们对逻辑回归（BCE损失）下的最优速率仍未知。作者旨在关闭这一间隙，确立精确的渐近速率。

Method: 1) 对循环实例进行更精细的分析，将下界改进为Ω(√(M/D))（当D<M²时）；2) 对所有D≥M²构造M覆盖路径的实例，实现超额误差下界Ω(M²/D)；3) 证明对任意固定分布，超额误差沿路径几何收缩，排除单个实例在所有深度同时见证多项式下界的可能性；4) 将回归下界迁移到logistic分类（logit-passing模型）：在这些示例上，逻辑路径在重缩放下等价于最小二乘路径。

Result: 证明了正确速率：深度小于M²时超额误差为常数，深度超过M²时为Θ(M²/D)。同时将相同的最优速率扩展到Bateni等人的logit-passing模型中基于BCE损失的逻辑分类问题上。

Conclusion: 论文彻底关闭了Kearns等人留下的上下界间隙，确立了网络化学习（覆盖路径深度D限制下的）信息聚合的最优收敛速率，并将结果从线性回归推广到逻辑分类。

Abstract: Building on the pioneering paper of Kearns, Roth, and Ryu (SODA'26), we study information aggregation in a networked learning model. The model captures a central pattern in agentic AI: each agent sees only part of the data and passes on only its own conclusion. Their model considers a linear regression problem with the mean squared error (MSE) loss. Agents sit in a DAG and each sees only a subset of the features and its parents' predictions, fits a linear predictor, and passes only its prediction forward. The benchmark is the full-feature learner that sees all raw features. A path of depth $D$ is $M$-covered if every block of $M$ consecutive agents collectively sees all raw features. Kearns, Roth, and Ryu proved that the excess mean squared error of the last agent on such a path is $O(M/\sqrt D)$, and gave a cyclic instance with excess error $Ω(M/D)$ for $D<M^2$.
  We close this gap: the correct rate is constant up to depth $M^2$, and $Θ(M^2/D)$ beyond it. We first give a sharper analysis of the cyclic instance and improve its lower bound to $Ω(\sqrt{M/D})$ for $D<M^2$. We then construct, for every depth $D\ge M^2$, an $M$-covered path of depth $D$ with excess error $Ω(M^2/D)$. The same instance gives the constant lower bound for all $D < M^2$. We also show that for any fixed distribution the excess error contracts geometrically along the path, ruling out any single instance that witnesses any polynomial lower bound at every depth.
  Finally, we prove the same optimal rate for logistic classification in the logit-passing model of Bateni et al., which considers the binary cross-entropy (BCE) loss. The same improved upper bound of $O(M^2/D)$ holds, and we transfer all the regression lower bounds by showing that on those examples the logistic path follows the least-squares path up to rescaling.

</details>


### [87] [Embedded Graph Flows for Categorical Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05328)
*Ethan Ma,Zihan Wang,Chris Siu Yeung Chow,Xinguo Feng,Qingqing Li,Rui Jiang,Naipeng Dong,Guangdong Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出嵌入式图流（EGF），一种用于类别化图生成的生成模型。EGF学习节点和无序边类别的连续嵌入，并利用置换等变的图Transformer将高斯噪声输送到这些学习到的端点，最终通过终端读出层将嵌入映射回离散图类别。在QM9上，EGF在全部四个指标上取得最优结果（FCD为0.150，优于DiGress的0.717和GruM的0.812）；在ZINC250k上保持了最低的NSPDK MMD，表明与参考分子局部子结构高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的类别化图生成模型通常将类别编码为固定的one-hot向量，这会在类别间施加一种人为的几何结构（即所有类别等距），缺乏对类别关系的灵活刻画。此外，已有方法（如基于类别扩散或桥接的基准）在生成质量上仍有提升空间。

Method: EGF通过三个关键组件实现生成：(1) 连续嵌入层，为节点类别和边类别学习可微的向量表示，避免one-hot带来的刚性距离假设；(2) 置换等变的图Transformer，将高斯噪声向学习到的嵌入端点进行传输，确保生成过程对节点排列不敏感；(3) 终端读出层，将传输后的嵌入映射回离散的图类别。

Result: 在QM9数据集上，EGF在全部四个报告指标上均优于类别扩散基线DiGress和桥接基线GruM，取得最佳结果；具体而言，FCD达到0.150，显著低于DiGress的0.717和GruM的0.812。在更大分子数据集ZINC250k上，EGF在使用NSPDK核的MMD指标上保持最低，表明生成的局部子结构分布与参考分子高度吻合。

Conclusion: 通过引入可学习的连续类别嵌入替代固定one-hot编码，并结合置换等变图Transformer进行噪声传输，EGF在多个分子图生成基准上展现了具有竞争力的性能，验证了该方法的有效性与可扩展性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Generating categorical graphs requires choosing node and edge types that form a coherent structure without depending on node order. Many graph generators encode categories as fixed one-hot vectors, which can impose an artificial geometry in which categories are equidistant. We propose Embedded Graph Flows (EGF), a generative model that learns continuous embeddings for node and unordered-edge categories and transports Gaussian noise towards these learnt endpoints using a permutation-equivariant graph transformer. A terminal readout maps the embeddings back to discrete graph categories. Across molecular benchmarks, EGF achieved competitive performance. On QM9, EGF gives the best result on all four reported metrics among the three methods, including a Fréchet ChemNet Distance (FCD) of 0.150, compared with 0.717 for the categorical-diffusion baseline DiGress and 0.812 for the bridge-based baseline GruM. When applied to larger molecules in ZINC250k, EGF retains the lowest maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) using the neighbourhood subgraph pairwise distance kernel (NSPDK), indicating close agreement with the local substructures of the reference molecules. Our code is available at https://github.com/Trusted-System-Lab/EGF.

</details>


### [88] [Variational Continuation for Double Pendulum Periodic Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05337)
*Leo Yao,Ziming Liu,Max Tegmark*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Hessian矩阵的方法来数值延拓动力系统中的周期轨道。该方法将周期轨道参数化为傅里叶级数，利用自动微分替代手工推导的雅可比矩阵，通过损失函数平坦方向（零特征值方向）确定延拓方向，实现无积分器、精确初始化不稳定不动点附近的振荡，并能高效检测轨道族交点与亚谐分岔。以双摆系统为例，展示了从不动点出发的完整周期轨道延拓，发现了文献中缺失的两摆质量块永不同时静止的周期轨道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统周期轨道数值延拓方法依赖手工推导的雅可比矩阵，过程繁琐且易出错，同时需要积分器进行数值积分。作者希望利用机器学习中的自动微分技术自动化这一过程，并利用损失景观的几何结构（Hessian矩阵的平坦方向）来引导延拓搜索，使周期轨道的寻找和延拓更加高效、自动化且精确。

Method: 1) 将周期轨道候选参数化为傅里叶级数（loop表示）；2) 定义损失函数度量该loop与物理微分方程的偏差；3) 利用自动微分计算损失函数的Hessian矩阵，替代手工推导的雅可比矩阵；4) 通过寻找Hessian的零特征值（平坦方向）确定延拓方向，实现无积分器的周期轨道延拓；5) 可用于从不稳定不动点附近精确初始化振荡，检测轨道族交点和亚谐分岔。

Result: 在周期双摆系统中完成了从不动点出发的完整周期轨道延拓，展示了沿轨道族的分岔现象并分类了周期轨道分支。特别地，发现了两摆质量块永不同时处于静止状态的周期轨道，据作者所知这在现有文献中是缺失的。

Conclusion: 基于Hessian和自动微分的方法能够高效、自动化地进行周期轨道的数值延拓，无需积分器和手工推导，并能发现新的动力学现象（如双摆中从未被报道的周期轨道类型），为周期轨道的系统搜索与分类提供了新的计算框架。

Abstract: We present a Hessian-based approach to numerically continue periodic orbits in dynamical systems. A loop (periodic orbit candidate) is parametrized as a Fourier series; a loss function is defined based on the deviation of the loop from the physical differential equations. Unlike previous work relying on hand-derived Jacobians, our method automates the process by leveraging automatic differentiation, a common machine learning technique. The continuation direction can be determined by the flat directions of the loss landscapes (directions with zero eigenvalues), making the search of periodic orbits efficient and guided. Our method is integrator-free, precisely initializes oscillations around unstable fixed points, and efficiently detects orbit family intersections and subharmonic bifurcations. As a demonstration, we present full continuations of periodic double pendulum oscillations from fixed points, showing bifurcations along orbit families and categorizing branches of periodic orbits. In particular, we find periodic orbits where both pendulum masses are never simultaneously at rest, which to our knowledge has been missing in the literature.

</details>


### [89] [Distill Globally, Adapt Locally: Reasoning Distillation and Product-Type Test-Time Training for Scalable Trade-Up Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05363)
*Siliang Liu,Mohammad Ghasemi,Sapan Patel,Amin Banitalebi-Dehkordi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两层级框架，将大语言模型（LLM）的推理能力蒸馏为高效的非生成式学生模型，用于升级推荐（trade-up recommendation）任务。第一层使用检索增强的少样本LLM教师生成结构化标签与自然语言推理，通过对齐和对比目标训练紧凑的嵌入对分类器；第二层通过产品类型的测试时训练（PT-TTT）优化轻量级类别特定适配器。最终15.5M参数的学生模型在8,352对人工标注基准上达到AUC 0.924，经PT-TTT提升至0.941，且推理速度比直接LLM推理快约5,000倍、成本低约10,000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 升级推荐需要识别在保留客户购买意图的同时提供更优功能的高质量替代品。LLM虽然能够推理这些细粒度区分，但直接对数亿级产品对应用LLM在运营上不可行，亟需一种既保留LLM推理能力又具备规模化效率的方法。

Method: 提出两层级框架：(1) 第一层（Level 1）：检索增强的少样本LLM教师生成结构化关系标签和自然语言推理，通过对齐与对比损失监督紧凑的嵌入对分类器，推理时仅依赖两个预计算的768维产品嵌入，无需LLM调用或文本生成；(2) 第二层（Level 2）：产品类型测试时训练（PT-TTT），利用少样本示范在冻结的学生模型上优化轻量级类别特定适配器，调整决策边界以适配不同产品类型的升级标准。

Result: 在8,352对人类标注基准上，15.5M参数的四分类推理蒸馏学生模型达到AUC 0.924（95% CI [0.918, 0.929]），优于仅用标签训练的学生（AUC 0.912）；PT-TTT将AUC从0.924提升至0.941，平均精确率从0.920提升至0.940；在100K对代理目录上，推理速度约为直接LLM推断的5,000倍，估算成本降低约10,000倍。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了通过蒸馏和测试时训练，可以将LLM的推理能力高效迁移到轻量级模型中，在保持高精度的同时实现百亿级产品对的规模化部署，为升级推荐系统中LLM知识获取与工业应用之间的权衡提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Trade-up recommendation identifies higher-quality alternatives that preserve a customer's purchase intent while offering upgraded benefits. Large language models (LLMs) can reason about such distinctions, but applying them directly to hundreds of millions of product pairs is operationally impractical. We introduce a two-level framework that distills LLM reasoning into an efficient non-generative student and adapts its decision boundary to product-type-specific trade-up criteria. At Level 1, a retrieval-augmented few-shot LLM teacher generates structured relation labels and natural-language rationales. These rationales supervise a compact embedding-pair classifier through alignment and contrastive objectives; at inference, the student uses only two precomputed 768-dimensional product embeddings, with no LLM calls or text generation. On a fixed human-annotated benchmark of 8,352 pairs, a 15.5M-parameter four-class reasoning-distilled student achieves AUC 0.924 (95% CI [0.918, 0.929]), compared with 0.912 for the four-class label-only student. At Level 2, product-type test-time training (PT-TTT) uses few-shot demonstrations to optimize lightweight category-specific adapters over the frozen student. PT-TTT improves AUC from 0.924 to 0.941 and average precision from 0.920 to 0.940. On a 100K-pair proxy catalog, the distilled student on a single eight-GPU machine is approximately 5,000x faster and 10,000x lower in estimated cost than direct LLM inference.

</details>


### [90] [RegionFed: Federated Learning for Personalized Query Understanding in Heterogeneous Retail Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05403)
*Quoc H. Nguyen,Ali Lafzi,Abhijeet Phatak,Siddharth Pratap Singh,Rohit Upadhyay,Yogananda Domlur Seetharama,Chittaranjan Tripathy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RegionFed，一个架构鲁棒的联邦学习框架，通过梯度级别的ℓ2冲突信号来诊断数据异构性、路由个性化策略并自适应控制个性化强度，从而在无需修改代码的情况下适用于T5、RoBERTa、CNN等不同架构，在多个数据集上达到92.27%准确率，接近中心化上界，同时提供差分隐私保证和O(1/√T)收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售搜索系统服务不同地理区域时存在显著的数据异构性，标准联邦学习会产生牺牲区域性能的全局模型，而现有参数级个性化联邦学习方法在现代Transformer上（如T5）因绑定嵌入和LayerNorm交互导致灾难性性能坍塌（低于10%准确率），亟需一种架构鲁棒的个性化联邦学习方法。

Method: RegionFed提出了梯度级别的联邦学习框架，使用区域梯度与全局梯度之间的ℓ2冲突作为统一信号，该信号同时用于：(i)诊断异构性程度；(ii)将每个区域路由到最廉价的充足个性化策略；(iii)自适应控制个性化强度。该方法将模型视为可微分黑盒，因此无需代码修改即可部署到不同架构（T5-Small、T5-3B、RoBERTa、CNN）上。

Result: 在三个公共数据集（Amazon ESCI、Amazon Reviews、LEAF-FEMNIST）和四种架构上，RegionFed-Meta达到92.27%准确率，与违反隐私的中心化上界（Centralized + Regional Weighting: 92.04%）仅差0.23个百分点（在1σ范围内），同时在参数级方法崩溃的Transformer上取得显著提升，并在CNN上保持一致改进，提供ε≈0.60的差分隐私保证和O(1/√T)收敛率。

Conclusion: RegionFed证明了梯度层面的个性化联邦学习是参数级方法在Transformer上坍塌问题的有效替代方案，通过统一的梯度冲突信号实现了异构性诊断、个性化策略选择和强度控制的解耦，在不修改模型架构的前提下达到接近中心化训练的性能，实现了隐私保护与区域个性化之间的良好平衡。

Abstract: Retail search systems serve diverse geographic regions with distinct query patterns, vocabularies, and product preferences, creating significant data heterogeneity that challenges both privacy-preserving training and model personalization. Federated learning offers a natural solution for privacy, but standard FL methods produce global models that sacrifice regional performance, while existing personalized FL approaches operate at the parameter level and catastrophically collapse on modern transformers (below 10\% accuracy on T5) due to tied embeddings and LayerNorm interactions. We introduce RegionFed, an \textit{architecture-robust} federated learning framework that sidesteps this failure by operating entirely at the gradient level. RegionFed uses the $\ell_2$ conflict between regional and global gradients as a unified signal that (i) diagnoses heterogeneity, (ii) routes each region to the cheapest sufficient personalization strategy, and (iii) adaptively controls personalization strength. Because it treats models as differentiable black boxes, RegionFed deploys on T5-Small, T5-3B, RoBERTa, and CNN with zero code changes, providing large gains on transformers (where parameter-level methods collapse) and consistent improvements on CNNs. Across three public datasets (Amazon ESCI, Amazon Reviews, LEAF-FEMNIST) and four architectures, RegionFed-Meta achieves 92.27\%, closing the gap to the privacy-violating centralized upper bound (Centralized + Regional Weighting: 92.04\%, $Δ$=0.23pp, within 1$σ$) while providing $(ε{\approx}0.60)$-differential privacy and $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$ convergence.

</details>

### [91] [DTM: Deterministic Approaches for Black-box Test Suite Minimization with Tree-based Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04205)
*Md Siam,Shartaz Sajid Nahid,Md Arif Hasan,Nurul Ahad Tawhid,Kazi Sakib*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DTM是一个确定性黑盒测试套件最小化框架，通过将测试用例转换为抽象语法树并采用四种树形相似度度量与三种确定性算法（Modified MST、Spectral Clustering、Dynamic Programming）进行子集选择，在Defects4J的16个Java项目（661个缺陷版本）上实现了0.74的平均准确率和0.98分钟的执行时间，优于所有现有方法且完全确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有效的黑盒测试套件最小化方法依赖进化搜索算法，引入非确定性导致多次运行结果不一致，降低了自动化测试管道的可靠性。亟需一种既能保持有效性又能保证确定性输出的测试套件最小化方案。

Method: DTM将测试用例转换为抽象语法树（AST），使用四种基于树的相似度度量计算两两相似性；在子集选择阶段采用三种确定性算法：改进的最小生成树（Modified MST）、谱聚类（Spectral Clustering）和动态规划（Dynamic Programming），从而在无需访问生产代码的情况下实现完全确定性的测试套件缩减。

Result: 在Defects4J的16个Java项目共661个缺陷版本上进行评估，DTM达到平均准确率0.74，执行时间仅0.98分钟，性能优于所有最先进的方法；且多次运行产生完全一致的结果，确保了完全确定性。

Conclusion: DTM成功证明了黑盒测试套件最小化可以在不牺牲有效性和效率的前提下实现完全确定性，解决了进化算法带来的非确定性可靠性问题，为自动化测试管道提供了可靠且可复现的测试套件缩减方案。

Abstract: Black-box Test Suite Minimization (TSM) techniques reduce testing costs without requiring access to production code. However, existing effective approaches rely on evolutionary search algorithms, introducing non-determinism that produces inconsistent results across runs, undermining reliability in automated testing pipelines. We propose DTM (Deterministic approaches for black-box Test suite Minimization), a framework that ensures deterministic test suite reduction while preserving effectiveness and efficiency. DTM converts test cases into Abstract Syntax Trees and computes pairwise similarities using four tree-based measures. For subset selection, it employs three deterministic algorithms: Modified Minimum Spanning Tree, Spectral Clustering, and Dynamic Programming. We evaluated DTM on 16 Java projects from Defects4J with 661 buggy versions. Experimental results show that DTM achieved an average accuracy of 0.74 with an execution time of just 0.98 minutes, outperforming all state-of-the-art approaches. Moreover, it consistently produced identical results across multiple runs, ensuring full determinism.

</details>


### [92] [Breaking the Alphabet: Rethinking File Ordering in Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04207)
*Md Shamimur Rahman,Zadia Codabux,Chanchal K. Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过一项覆盖1,355名开发者、182个开源项目的大规模混合方法调查，首次系统研究Pull Request中文件排序方式对代码评审效果的影响。研究发现仅10.2%的评审者认为字母序排列最优，57.6%认为其增加了上下文切换和评审疲劳，63.9%担心默认排序会导致遗漏bug，66%的评审者期望具备依赖感知分组和自定义文件排序功能的工具改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代码评审研究主要关注文件位置如何影响评审者注意力，但关于文件排序如何影响认知负荷和感知评审彻底性仍然不清楚；同时主流的评审工具默认按字母顺序排列文件，优先考虑可预测性而非上下文相关性，有必要检验这种默认设计是否符合人类认知。

Method: 该研究采用混合方法设计，对来自182个广泛使用的开源项目的1,355名专业开发者进行大规模问卷调查，分析文件排序对评审行为、代码理解和感知有效性的影响，并对评审者反馈进行定量与定性结合的分析。

Result: 研究发现：仅10.2%的评审者认为字母序排序最优；57.6%认为它增加了上下文切换、打断了逻辑推理并导致评审疲劳；63.9%担心默认排序导致漏掉bug；66%的评审者要求依赖感知分组和可自定义文件排序的工具改进。研究还识别了多文件评审中的关键挑战并收集了开发者对改进工具设计的期望。

Conclusion: 研究表明当前文件排序默认方案与人类认知存在错位，需要采用以评审者为中心的界面设计，使工具行为更好地与人类认知机制对齐，包括依赖感知的文件分组和自定义排序能力。

Abstract: Effective code review is central to maintaining software quality, yet there is limited research about how the ordering of changed files in Pull Requests (PRs) influences review effectiveness. Most popular code review tools default to alphabetical ordering, favoring predictability over contextual relevance. While prior studies examined how file position shapes reviewer attention, it remains unclear how such ordering influences cognitive load and perceived review thoroughness. This study presents the first large-scale survey of 1,355 professional developers across 182 widely used open-source projects to investigate how file ordering impacts review behavior, comprehension, and perceived effectiveness. Our mixed-methods analysis reveals that only 10.2% of reviewers consider alphabetical ordering optimal, underscoring a cognitive misalignment in their interpretation of code changes. Although some developers appreciate its predictability, more than half (57.6%) report that it increases context switching, disrupts logical reasoning, and contributes to review fatigue, and 63.9% expressed concern that the default ordering may cause them to miss bugs. We further identify key challenges in multi-file reviews and elicit developers' expectations for improved tooling, including dependency-aware grouping and customizable file ordering (requested by 66% of reviewers). These findings highlight the need for reviewer-centric interface designs that better align tool behavior with human cognition.

</details>


### [93] [AI Writes Code, Humans Pay the Debt. An Empirical Study on the Sustainability and Evolution of Agent-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04208)
*Antonino Coppola,Matteo Esposito,Rick Kazman,Valentina Lenarduzzi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一项大规模实证研究计划，旨在系统性地调查生成式AI编码代理对软件质量的影响，重点考察问题定位准确性、技术债务引入情况及其长期演化趋势，通过与人类开发者实际提交进行对比分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI编码代理在软件工程中日益普及，短期生产率提升已被证实，但其对软件质量和长期技术债务的系统性影响尚不清楚。作者旨在填补这一实证研究空白，为AI编码代理的负责任采用提供数据支撑。

Method: 采用大规模软件仓库挖掘研究方法，基于SQuaD数据集构建62.8万条问题票据的候选集，使用多种LLM代理生成实现并进行系统性基准测试筛选，随后通过静态分析指标和工具将这些实现与开发者的真实提交在提交级别和发布级别进行对比。

Result: 预期结果包括：提供代理驱动开发引入权衡的实证证据（定位准确性差异、技术债务引入差异、长期演化分歧），揭示不同LLM间的性能变异性。

Conclusion: 该工作预期将丰富对含代理软件演化的理解，并为在软件开发中更负责任地采用AI代理提供信息支持。

Abstract: Context. The increasing adoption of Generative AI coding agents in software engineering is transforming how developers implement and maintain code. While these systems provide short-term productivity benefits, their long-term impact on software quality and technical debt remains unclear. Aim. We aim to investigate how agent-generated code affects software quality, focusing on issue localization accuracy, the introduction of technical debt, and its evolution over time. Method. We will conduct a large-scale mining software repositories study using the SQuaD dataset, employing a candidate set of 628k issue tickets. We will generate agent-based implementations for these issues, and compare them with the actual commits done by developers using static analysis metrics and tools. We will analyze differences at the commit level and across releases, and we will consider multiple LLM-based Agents selected through a systematic benchmarking strategy. Expected Results. We expect to provide empirical evidence on the trade-offs introduced by agent-based development, including differences in localization accuracy, variations in technical debt introduction, and potential divergence in long-term evolution. We expect the results to highlight variability across LLMs, to enrich our understanding of software evolution with Agents, and to inform more responsible adoption of Agents in software development.

</details>


### [94] [The Prompt Triangle: A Registered Report on Prompts as Hybrid Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04209)
*Shalini Chakraborty,Jan-Philipp Steghöfer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个注册报告（Stage 1 协议），旨在通过受控实验（n=30）、社区上传和挖掘数据，研究AI辅助编码中提示词如何作为软件工程工件演化，以及提示词与需求工程（RE）活动的对齐程度是否能预测开发成果。研究预注册了四个假设，分别涉及提示词演化、开发者特征、RE活动对齐和时间模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI编码助手日益重要，但缺乏关于提示词作为软件工程工件的实证证据。已有研究将提示词概念化为混合工件（结合需求意图和解决方案指导），本文在此框架（Prompt Triangle）基础上开展验证性研究，以填补提示词演化及其与需求工程对齐如何影响开发成果的实证空白。

Method: 采用注册报告（Registered Report）形式，预注册四项假设。研究方法采用三源多方法的混合设计：（1）受控实验（n=30）；（2）社区上传数据；（3）挖掘数据。使用双编码（dual-coding）方法对提示词进行分类和分析，以检验提示模式是否能预测开发成功。

Result: 本文是Stage 1注册报告协议，主要产出包括：预注册的四个假设（提示词演化、开发者特征、RE活动对齐、时间模式），以及一套结合受控实验、社区数据和挖掘数据的多方法验证研究设计。论文尚未报告具体实证结果（属于协议阶段）。

Conclusion: 该研究旨在通过严格预注册的验证性研究，建立提示词作为软件工程工件在RE活动中的实证基础，探索提示词演化规律及其与开发成果的预测关系，为AI辅助软件开发的工程实践提供证据支持。

Abstract: AI-based coding assistants are transforming software development by shifting effort from writing code to crafting prompts that guide code generation. Despite their growing importance, little empirical evidence exists on how prompts function as software engineering artifacts. Building on prior work framing prompts as mixed artifacts combining requirement intent and solution guidance, we build on the Prompt Triangle, a conceptual model describing prompts along three components: Functionality and Quality (requirements), General Solutions (architectural guidance), and Specific Solutions (implementation constraints). This registered report presents the Stage 1 protocol for a confirmatory study investigating how prompts evolve and how their alignment with requirements engineering (RE) activities predicts development outcomes. We preregister four hypotheses examining prompt evolution, developer characteristics, RE activity alignment, and temporal patterns. We employ a controlled experiment (n=30), community uploads, and mined data using dual-coding to test whether prompting patterns predict development success.

</details>


### [95] [SH-PDOPS: AI-Driven Cloud Native Enterprise Reliability Framework for Predictive Analytics and Intelligent DevOps Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04210)
*Ayushman Bosu Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种AI驱动的云原生企业可靠性框架，整合机器学习、Kubernetes编排、可观测性流水线和自动化事件响应机制，以提升预测分析、智能DevOps自动化和系统韧性。实验表明该框架能提升运维效率、减少停机时间并增强企业环境的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分布式基础设施日益复杂，传统可靠性工程方法难以应对动态环境中的故障预测、异常检测和自动化运维需求，缺乏将AI与DevOps方法论有效融合以支持自适应和自主基础设施管理的方案。

Method: 构建一个集成多种云原生技术的综合性可靠性框架，具体包括：1）集成机器学习模型用于预测性故障检测和异常监控；2）利用Kubernetes实现容器编排与自愈基础设施；3）构建可观测性流水线实现全链路监控；4）设计自动化事件响应机制；5）优化CI/CD流程以支持智能DevOps自动化。

Result: 实验评估表明，该框架在运维效率、系统可靠性和可扩展性方面均有显著提升，具体体现为减少系统停机时间、改善异常检测能力，并能够有效支撑企业级大规模分布式环境。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种将人工智能与DevOps方法相结合的实用路径，可实现自适应、自主化的基础设施管理，为云原生企业环境下的可靠性工程实践提供了可行的技术方案和参考架构。

Abstract: This paper presents an AI-driven cloud-native enterprise reliability framework designed to improve predictive analytics, intelligent DevOps automation, and system resilience in modern distributed infrastructures. The proposed framework integrates machine learning models, Kubernetes orchestration, observability pipelines, and automated incident response mechanisms to enhance reliability engineering practices. The study explores predictive failure detection, anomaly monitoring, self-healing infrastructure, and CI/CD optimization using cloud-native technologies. Experimental evaluation demonstrates improved operational efficiency, reduced downtime, and enhanced scalability for enterprise environments. The framework provides a practical approach for combining artificial intelligence with DevOps methodologies to achieve adaptive and autonomous infrastructure management.

</details>


### [96] [Data-Related Challenges and Requirements for Event Log Generation in Process Mining: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04211)
*Ghita El Alaoui Talibi,Oleksandr Kosenkov,Anastasija Nikiforova*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统文献综述，识别出事件日志生成过程中29项数据相关挑战，可归为5类基本需求类别，并发现现有解决方案已嵌入了数据工程和领域知识工程技术，为流程挖掘领域的研究者和实践者提供了全面的趋势理解和改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流程挖掘已成为支撑业务流程改进和未来数字经济的关键技术，而数据质量是社区面临的最紧迫挑战。然而，事件日志生成作为流程挖掘的第一阶段，其与数据工程和需求工程的关系长期未得到足够关注，存在研究空白。

Method: 采用系统文献综述（Systematic Literature Review）方法，系统性地梳理事件日志生成中的相关数据挑战、需求与解决方案，并对其中的关系进行分析和归类。

Result: 识别出29项可用5类基本需求类别解决的广泛挑战；现有解决方案已在一定程度上集成了数据工程和领域知识工程技术用于事件日志生成。

Conclusion: 该综述可帮助研究者和实践者理解事件日志生成的当前趋势，并通过利用数据和需求工程技术来改进日志生成，最终提升流程挖掘的整体效果。

Abstract: Process mining is becoming an essential technology that not only supports the improvement of business processes but also enables the software technologies underlying the future digital economy. Among the multiple challenges discussed by the process mining community, data quality is emerging as the most pressing one. However, insufficient attention has been paid to event log generation as the very first phase of process mining and to its relationship with data engineering and requirements engineering. To address this gap, we conducted a systematic literature review of data-related challenges, requirements, and solutions in event log generation. Our results show that a wide range of 29 challenges can be addressed with 5 basic categories of requirements. Existing solutions already embed some form of data engineering and domain knowledge engineering techniques for event log generation. This review can support researchers and practitioners in understanding current trends in event log generation for process mining and in leveraging data and requirements engineering techniques to improve log generation and, ultimately, process mining outcomes.

</details>


### [97] [Big Questions on Software Architecture: Report of the ICSE 2026 BoF on Software Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04212)
*Davide Taibi,Patricia Lago,Henry Muccini*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个社区倡议的阶段性成果，旨在识别和讨论软件架构（SA）研究中的重大科学问题。该倡议分两个会议（2026年ICSE和ICSA）进行，本文汇报了第一阶段的讨论结果，后续结果待补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件架构（SA）的重要性日益凸显，涵盖技术对社会与地球的影响、人类与技术之间的平衡、灵活性与混乱的权衡、经济便利与数字自主的关系等。然而，SA研究社区需要反思：哪些是值得科学研究的重大问题，哪些仅仅是技术趋势或一时热潮。作者认为有必要通过社区协作来厘清这些边界。

Method: 采用社区倡议的方式，组织了两轮专门的研讨会议（2026年于ICSE和ICSA大会举办），通过社区讨论来收集和识别SA研究中的重大开放问题。本文报告前一轮会议（第一场）的结果。

Result: 论文呈现了第一场社区研讨会议（ICSE）中收集到的关于SA重大研究问题的讨论结果。第二场会议（ICSA）的结果尚未完成，将在后续中补充呈现。

Conclusion: SA研究社区需要持续反思和定义其核心研究议程，区分真正值得长期投入的科学问题与短期的技术潮流。本文以社区协作的方式迈出了第一步，但仍需通过后续研讨（如ICSA会议）进一步完善和汇总观点。

Abstract: Software Architecture (SA) is more important than ever, from the need to master the pervasive impact of technology in society and planet earth, to balancing the role of humans versus technology, flexibility versus chaos, economic convenience versus digital autonomy. Despite SA importance, we feel that the SA research community needs to reflect on what are the big questions that deserve scientific research, and what are instead technology trends or hypes. In this paper, we report the results of a community initiative aiming to identify and discuss the big questions in SA research. Organised in two sessions hosted in 2026 at the ICSE and ICSA conferences, respectively, this paper reports on the results collected during the first session, and looks forward to complementing them with the results of the second one. To be continued ...

</details>


### [98] [Toward Model-Driven Digital Twin Configuration: Separating Structure Semantics and Runtime with SysML SAREF and Ditto](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04213)
*Andrey Sadovykh,Matthew Rusakov,Kirill Korikov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出将能源社区数字孪生的配置生成任务拆分为三个互补工具（SysML建模、SAREF4ENER语义词汇、Eclipse Ditto运行时平台），并给出从SysML注释模型推导Ditto配置的概念性规则，以改善可追溯性与配置一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源社区的数字孪生配置目前多为手工完成，存在字段名随意、单位隐式、缺乏设备间关系的机器可读描述等问题，导致其他系统无法可靠解释配置。这阻碍了系统的互操作性与可追溯性。

Method: 提出将任务拆分给三个各司其职的工具：SysML建模系统结构、SAREF4ENER提供能源领域标准语义词汇、Eclipse Ditto（基于W3C Web-of-Things Thing Description）作为运行时数字孪生平台。作者给出（尚未可执行的）概念性规则，用于从以该词汇注释的SysML模型中推导Ditto配置，并在Modelio中对温室能源社区节点进行了示例说明。

Result: 通过温室能源社区节点的示例展示，论证该拆分方式相比手工编写配置能改善可追溯性（traceability）与配置一致性（configuration consistency），并为互操作性建立语义钩子（semantic hooks）。贡献范围限定在设计期配置层面。

Conclusion: 该工作属于立场论文（position paper），提出概念性框架而非完整实现，并讨论了其局限性；结论认为这种三方分工的方式是通往更可靠、可互操作的能源社区数字孪生配置的有前景方向。

Abstract: Energy communities, neighbourhoods that jointly produce, store, and consume electricity, increasingly rely on \emph{digital twins}: live software copies of physical devices such as a solar panel, a battery, or a heater, kept in sync for monitoring and control. Setting up such a twin is today mostly manual: an engineer hand-writes a configuration whose field names are arbitrary, whose units are implicit, and which carries no machine-readable description of how devices relate, so other systems cannot reliably interpret it. This position paper argues that the task should be split across three complementary tools, each doing what it does best: a modelling language for the system's structure (SysML), a standard energy vocabulary for its meaning (SAREF4ENER), and a digital-twin platform for the running representation (Eclipse Ditto, via the W3C Web-of-Things Thing Description). We give conceptual rules (not yet an executable generator) for deriving a Ditto configuration from a SysML model annotated with that vocabulary, illustrate them on a greenhouse energy-community node modelled in Modelio, and argue that the split improves traceability and configuration consistency, and establishes the semantic hooks needed for interoperability, over hand-written alternatives. We scope the contribution to design-time configuration and discuss its limits.

</details>


### [99] [A Mixed-Method Empirical Study of LLM Assistance in Software Engineering Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04214)
*Pamali D. Weerasinghe,Roshan N. Rajapakse,Isuru Dharmadasa,Chamath Keppitiyagama*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法实证研究（初步调查n=157与准实验n=20）比较了大学一年级和四年级学生在LLM辅助与非辅助条件下完成软件工程任务的表现，发现LLM辅助的收益具有任务依赖性，受开发者经验水平和验证实践的中介作用，而非仅仅取决于生成速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs被越来越多地整合到软件开发流程中，但现有讨论往往未区分任务类型、开发者资历和验证需求对LLM效果的影响。作者希望填补这一空白，通过实证研究揭示LLM辅助软件工程中这些因素的调节作用。

Method: 采用混合方法设计，包含两个阶段：(1) 第一阶段为初步调查（n=157），表征两组学生（一年级与四年级）的LLM暴露程度、依赖程度和信任校准；(2) 第二阶段为基于任务的准实验（n=20），从两届学生中抽取目的样本，在AI辅助与非AI条件下比较实现、约束驱动算法选择和架构推理三类结构化任务的表现；通过屏幕录制和行为跟踪进行定性与定量分析。

Result: 调查显示LLM普遍被采用且验证工作量大，两个群体在约束密集型场景中对LLM能力的感知存在差异。准实验表明AI辅助改变了工作流结构，参与者常采用AI优先的任务入口、复制粘贴集成和AI中介调试模式，而非AI工作流更多依赖文档、先前模板和迭代试错细化。

Conclusion: LLM辅助的收益并非仅由生成速度决定，而是取决于任务类型，并受开发者专业水平和验证实践的调节作用影响。这提示需要在软件工程教育和工具设计中考虑任务与用户特性的差异化适配。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into software development workflows, yet their effects are often discussed without distinguishing between task types, developer seniority, and verification demands. This paper presents a mixed-method empirical study of LLM-assisted software engineering with first-year and fourth-year undergraduates. Phase 1 is a preliminary survey (n=157) that characterizes LLM exposure, reliance, and trust calibration among the two groups. Phase 2 is a task-based quasi-experiment with a purposive sample from both cohorts (n=20). Here, we compare AI-assisted and non-AI conditions on a structured set of software engineering tasks spanning implementation, constraint-driven algorithm selection, and architectural reasoning. We then analyze performance outcomes alongside behavioral traces captured via screen recording and a qualitative coding process. Survey results indicate widespread LLM adoption and substantial verification effort, alongside cohort differences in perceived LLM capability for constraint-heavy scenarios. The quasi-experiment further shows that AI assistance changes workflow structure. For example, participants frequently adopt AI-first task entry, copy-transfer integration, and AI-mediated debugging, whereas non-AI workflows rely more on documentation, prior templates, and iterative trial-error refinement. Overall, our findings suggest that the benefits of LLM assistance are task-dependent and mediated by expertise and verification practices, rather than by generation speed alone.

</details>


### [100] [A systematic literature review on logging smell detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04215)
*Nora Madi,Manal Binkhonain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统文献综述（SLR）对21项关于日志气味检测的研究进行了梳理，发现该领域研究分散且缺乏统一的基准和标准化的评估方法，指出未来应开发更完善的工具、引入大语言模型并构建标准化数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日志是软件开发中的重要组成部分，但糟糕的日志实践会产生"日志气味"（logging smells），影响日志有用性甚至造成问题。当前该领域缺乏系统的综述，作者希望通过SLR更好地理解已有研究中的自动检测技术、数据集和评估方法。

Method: 采用系统文献综述（SLR）方法，对21项聚焦于日志气味检测的研究进行分析。具体包括：定义关键的日志相关术语、将气味类型映射到已有分类体系、考察各研究中的检测技术、数据集和评估策略。

Result: 研究发现该领域研究仍呈现分散和不一致的状态：缺乏通用基准（benchmark）和标准化评估手段，导致不同研究难以比较；各研究在针对的气味类型和数量上存在差异，分类口径不统一。

Conclusion: 日志气味的研究与检测仍有较大改进空间。作者指出了若干挑战，并建议未来应开发更优质的工具、探索利用大语言模型（LLMs）的能力，以及构建更标准化的评估数据集，以推动该领域走向成熟。

Abstract: Context:Logging is an important part of software development that helps developers monitor systems, understand behavior, and fix problems. But when logging is done poorly, it can introduce logging smells, which are defects that reduce the usefulness of logs or even make them problematic. Objective:This study looks at how logging smells are currently detected. The goal is to better understand the existing research on automatic detection techniques, datasets, and evaluation methods. Method:We conducted a systematic literature review (SLR) of 21 studies focused on detecting logging smells. In this review, we define key logging-related terms, identify and map the types of smells to an existing taxonomy, and examine the detection techniques, datasets, and evaluation strategies used across the studies. Results:We found that the research is still scattered and inconsistent. For example, there is no common benchmark or standardized approach for evaluating results, making it difficult to compare studies. In addition, we observe inconsistencies in the way log smells are addressed, as studies differ in the types and number of smells they target. Conclusion:There is still room for improvement in how logging smells are studied and detected. We point out several challenges and suggest future directions, such as developing better tools, using large language models (LLMs), and building more standardized datasets for evaluation.

</details>


### [101] [Engineering as Code: Bringing Software Engineering Methodology to Engineering Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04216)
*Song Difei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出"Engineering as Code (EaC)"范式，将工程设计表达为文本原生的声明式语言（ADL），并通过自动化规则引擎（ESA）实现设计时合规检查。实验证明在跨领域样例中可在200ms内检查69条规则，违规注入实验达到100%检出率和零误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在代码生成等可验证领域取得了显著进展，但在建筑、机械工程、暖通空调等工程设计任务上仍受限。作者认为根本原因不是模型能力不足，而是缺乏可计算的设计表示基础——缺少能被机器消费、验证和版本化的"Design as Code"架构。现有CAD/BIM系统将设计逻辑与几何耦合导致质量保证后移，自动化合规检查存在几何误报和命名依赖的重建开销问题。

Method: 提出Engineering as Code (EaC)范式：将工程表达为文本原生的声明式语言，以自动化规则引擎、版本控制和包管理作为质量门。具体包括：(1)信息表示假设——提出工程AI瓶颈源于缺失可计算设计表示而非模型能力；(2)ADL装配定义语言，以Part为原子，划分为三个正交子语言：PDL（零件定义）、PML（零件配合）、PLL（零件布局）；(3)ESA工程静态分析——将合规检查从下游审查转为设计时执行，基于语义类别（族类型、配合关系）而非几何进行，消除误报和命名依赖。

Result: 原型系统验证了ADL的表达能力和ESA检测能力，覆盖三个跨领域样例（电信机架扩展、模块化数据中心、机械键盘），在200ms内检查69条规则。受控违规注入实验达到100%检出率和零误报。SD-HWE-Bench基准设计完成，作为假设的实证测试平台。

Conclusion: 工程AI的发展瓶颈在于缺少可计算的工程设计表示，而非模型能力本身。EaC范式通过文本原生声明式语言和设计时静态分析，为工程设计AI提供了类似软件工程中"代码即基础设施"的可计算基础，有望推动该领域的AI能力突破。

Abstract: Large language models have made significant progress in verifiable domains such as code generation and chip design, yet remain limited on engineering design tasks in fields like architecture, mechanical engineering, and HVAC. This paper argues the root cause is not model capability but the absence of a "Design as Code" computable foundation: design representations that can be consumed, verified, and versioned by machines.
