Existing CAD/BIM systems couple design logic with geometry, causing quality assurance to drift downstream; Automated Compliance Checking (ACC) suffers from geometric false positives and naming-dependent model reconstruction overhead. We propose the Engineering as Code (EaC) paradigm: expressing engineering design as text-native declarative language with automated rule engines, version control, and package management as quality gates. Core contributions: (1) the Information Representation Hypothesis, arguing the engineering AI bottleneck stems from missing computable design representations rather than model capability; (2) ADL (Assembly Definition Language) with Part as atom, organized into three orthogonal sub-languages -- PDL (Part Definition), PML (Part Mating), and PLL (Part Layout); (3) ESA (Engineering Static Analysis), shifting compliance checking from downstream review to design-time, operating on semantic categories (Family types, Mate relations) rather than geometry, eliminating false positives and naming dependencies. A prototype validates ADL expressiveness and ESA detection across three cross-domain samples (telecom rack expansion, modular datacenter, mechanical keyboard), checking 69 rules in under 200ms. A controlled violation injection experiment achieves 100% detection rate with zero false positives. The SD-HWE-Bench benchmark design is complete as an empirical testbed for the hypothesis.

</details>


### [102] [A Governance Methodology Layer for AI-Assisted Software Development: Defect Taxonomy, Controlled Ablation, and Process-Over-Capability Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04218)
*Sungjin Kwon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对自主编码AI代理输出虽通过语法检查但缺乏语义正确性的问题，提出了一个治理框架：包含缺陷分类学、运行时解耦的治理门、方法论即代码（methodology-as-code）架构，并通过对照消融实验（N=5工件）验证了结构化审查流程相比非结构化审查能显著提升关键缺陷覆盖率（宽松标准62% vs 50%，严格标准25% vs 0%）。结论表明审查流程设计而非模型能力是决定AI辅助软件开发中关键缺陷覆盖的主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主编码代理能以极高速度产生通过编译、类型安全和CI等语法检查的代码，但语法正确性不等于语义正确性——设计边界、安全不变量和维护性契约在自动化管道中对结构不可见。现有自动化检查无法覆盖此类语义层面缺陷，需要一种超越静态分析的系统化审查治理方案。

Method: 论文提出四项贡献：(1)基于五个AI代理权限与治理模块的缺陷类分类学，区分静态分析与语义审查可检测的结构性缺陷；(2)运行时解耦的治理门——一种基于文件协议的审查机制，读取生成器输出并发出结构化裁决，无需API耦合，跨代码生成工具可移植；(3)方法论即代码的形式化——将验证协议编码为版本控制、可执行、跨平台的两个层架构（可移植方法论层与主机特定自动化层分离）；(4)受控消融实验（E-ablation，5个工件、8项独立真值），在同一工件上比较框架化结构审查与token匹配的非结构化审查提示。

Result: 结构化审查条件在宽松真值召回率上达到62%（非结构化条件为50%），严格召回率为25%（非结构化条件为0%），且严重程度分级更一致，而非结构化条件倾向于夸大严重度。两种条件都遗漏了人类QA评审员识别的文档质量缺陷，表明结构化AI审查与人工流程检查存在互补性。

Conclusion: 在该样本范围内，实验结果支持论文的核心论点：审查流程设计而非评审模型能力，才是AI辅助软件开发中关键缺陷覆盖的主导决定因素。结构化治理框架可显著改善语义缺陷的检测效果，但AI审查与人工流程审查应视为互补而非替代关系。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents produce output that passes syntactic checks -- compilation, type safety, CI -- at high velocity. Yet syntactic correctness does not imply semantic correctness: design boundaries, security invariants, and maintainability contracts remain structurally invisible to automated pipelines. This paper makes four contributions toward closing this gap. First, we present a defect-class taxonomy grounded in five AI agent permission and governance modules, distinguishing defects structurally detectable by static analysis from those requiring semantic review. Second, we describe a runtime-decoupled governance gate -- a file-based protocol that reads generator output and emits a structured verdict without API coupling, making it portable across code-generation tools. Third, we formalize methodology-as-code: expressing a verification protocol as a version-controlled, executable, cross-platform artifact with a two-layer architecture separating portable methodology from host-specific automation. Fourth, we report a controlled ablation experiment (E-ablation, N=5 artifacts, 8-item independent ground truth) comparing harness-structured review against a token-matched unstructured review prompt on the same artifacts. The structured condition records 62% lenient ground-truth recall against 50% for the unstructured condition, with 25% strict recall against 0%, and applies more consistent severity grades where the unstructured condition inflates severity. Both conditions miss document-quality defects identified by a human QA reviewer, indicating a complementarity between structured AI review and human process inspection. Within the bounds of this sample, the results are consistent with the thesis that review-process design, rather than reviewer-model capability, is the dominant factor in critical-defect coverage for AI-assisted software development.

</details>


### [103] [Large Language Models for Fuzz Testing in Microservices: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04219)
*Ying Song,Ke Ping,Yuqing Wang,Xiaozhou Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对LLM辅助的微服务模糊测试进行了系统性文献综述，分析了2024至2026年间20项主要研究，提出LLM角色的分类体系，并总结了评估实践与开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统依赖异构API，其组合输入空间和有状态依赖使传统模糊测试面临挑战；LLM为模糊测试引入了基于规范的语义推理能力，但缺乏对这些LLM辅助方法的系统梳理和综合评估，亟需一篇系统性文献综述来整合研究现状。

Method: 遵循既定的SLR指南，对2024至2026年间发表的20项主要研究进行系统分析，从LLM的角色分类、集成架构、评估实践等维度进行归纳，提出LLM角色与集成方式的分类体系。

Result: LLM主要作为黑盒模糊测试中的语义输入生成器使用，并正逐步向基于智能体（agent-based）和检索增强（retrieval-augmented）架构演变，提升了有效输入生成能力并适度增加了覆盖率和漏洞检测。但评估仍呈异构性：基准标准化不足、成本报告稀缺、偏向单一服务实验，与真实多服务系统存在差距。

Conclusion: 该综述提供了LLM角色与集成的分类体系、评估实践的综合视图以及开放挑战到研究方向的映射，为微服务中LLM驱动模糊测试的设计与部署提供支撑。

Abstract: Microservice systems (MSS) increasingly rely on heterogeneous APIs whose combinatorial input space and stateful dependencies challenge traditional fuzz testing. Meanwhile, Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently been introduced to enhance fuzzing with semantic reasoning over specifications, inputs, and runtime feedback. This paper presents a systematic literature review (SLR) of LLM-assisted fuzz testing for microservices to synthesise how LLMs are applied, evaluated, and what challenges remain. Following established SLR guidelines, we analyze 20 primary studies published between 2024 and 2026. Results show LLMs are mainly used as semantic input generators in black-box fuzzing, with a growing shift towards agent-based and retrieval-augmented architectures, improving valid input generation and modestly increasing coverage and vulnerability detection. However, evaluation remains heterogeneous, with limited benchmark standardization, scarce cost reporting, and a bias toward single-service experiments, highlighting a gap with real-world multi-service systems. This review provides a taxonomy of LLM roles and integrations, a consolidated view of evaluation practices, and a mapping of open challenges to research directions, supporting the design and deployment of LLM-driven fuzzing in microservices.

</details>


### [104] [Robustness and Trade-offs for Code LLMs on Protected Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04220)
*Jin Wen,Yuejun Guo,Yujie Ma,Qiang Hu,Maxime Cordy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对7个代码大语言模型在混淆代码翻译与补全任务上进行了基于执行的研究，涵盖4种源语言、5种混淆方法和3种推理协议（明文、混淆、去混淆），发现对混淆代码直接推理往往优于先还原再推理，模型能力是主要决定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码LLM越来越多地被用于可能被故意混淆的软件工件上（如知识产权保护、逆向工程抵抗等），而去混淆通常被视为下游分析前的预处理步骤，但其对代码LLM管线的实际价值尚未跨模型和防护方法被系统性验证。

Method: 采用基于执行（execution-based）的评估方法，对7个代码LLM进行受保护代码的翻译和补全任务测试，覆盖C++、Go、Java、JavaScript四种源语言，五种不同的混淆方法，以及三种推理协议（明文、混淆、去混淆）。通过受控基准测试比较不同模型在不同条件下的Pass@1表现。

Result: 高能力模型（如GPT-4.1、Qwen3-Coder-30B）在混淆翻译输入上仍保持约90%的Pass@1；直接对混淆代码推理通常匹配或超过对还原代码的推理。同模型还原虽能恢复部分混淆导致的失败，但会降低许多原本成功的案例，低能力模型的净损失最大。模型能力是主要因素，源语言和混淆方法是次要但一致的影响因素。

Conclusion: 研究支持模型感知的管线设计，受保护代码工作流的评估应主要采用基于执行的指标而非仅用静态相似度衡量。

Abstract: Code large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used on software artifacts that may be intentionally obfuscated for intellectual-property protection, reverse-engineering resistance, or controlled access. In reverse engineering and security analysis, deobfuscation is commonly treated as the preprocessing step before downstream analysis or inference, yet its utility for code LLM pipelines has not been systematically validated across models and protection methods. We present an execution-based study of seven code LLMs on protected-code translation and completion, spanning source programs from C++, Go, Java, and JavaScript, five obfuscation methods, and three inference protocols: plain, obfuscated, and deobfuscated. Our results show that direct inference on obfuscated code often matches or exceeds inference on restored code. In this controlled benchmark, higher-capability models such as GPT-4.1 and Qwen3-Coder-30B retain about 90% Pass@1 on obfuscated translation inputs, indicating that explicit restoration is often unnecessary. Same-model restoration does recover some obfuscation-induced failures, but it also degrades many cases that already succeed, with lower-capability models showing the largest net losses. Across settings, model capability is the primary factor, while source language and obfuscation method have secondary but consistent effects. Overall, our findings support model-aware pipeline design and indicate that protected-code workflows should be evaluated primarily with execution-based metrics rather than static similarity alone.

</details>


### [105] [Automated Deployment of Real-Time Tasks for Phased Execution on Scratchpad-Based Multicore Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04221)
*Konstantin Dudzik,Maximilian Kirschner,Jürgen Becker*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模型的部署方法，自动化地将实时应用适配为阶段执行（phased execution）模式，以解决多核实时系统中共享内存争用导致的干扰问题。该方法从应用程序二进制中提取所需信息，最小化对应用实现的修改，并通过集成工具链提供运行时环境来协调查硬件执行。在RISC-V多核系统上的航空电子案例研究中验证了其有效性和关键工作原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多核实时系统面临共享内存访问争用带来的干扰问题，阶段执行方法虽能解决该问题，但内存阶段的管理与调度复杂性阻碍了其在实际应用中的采用，亟需一种自动化部署方法来降低这一复杂度。

Method: 提出模型驱动的自动化部署方法：(1) 通过集成工具链扩展应用程序添加必要的内存阶段；(2) 提供运行时环境在目标硬件平台上协调执行；(3) 直接从生成的应用程序二进制中提取所需信息，使工具对应用实现透明，仅需最小改动。

Result: 在RISC-V多核系统上使用航空电子案例研究验证了所提方法，展示了其关键组件和运行原理，证明了该方法在实际嵌入式系统中的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 基于模型的自动化部署方法有效克服了阶段执行在实时应用中部署的复杂性问题，通过从二进制层面提取信息和自动化工具链支持，实现了对应用最小侵入的部署流程，为多核实时系统的实际应用提供了一条通路。

Abstract: The increasing throughput demands in real-time systems and the consolidation of functionality on few, high-performance platforms are driving a shift towards parallel architectures. A key challenge for multicore real-time systems is the interference from contention for access to shared memory. Phased execution approaches address this challenge by restricting shared memory access to mutually exclusive memory phases, while computation is limited to memories local to each core. However, the complexity of managing and scheduling said memory phases hinders the adoption of phased execution in real-time applications. To address this challenge, we propose a model-based deployment methodology that automates the process of adapting applications to phased execution. We present an integrated tool-based deployment process that extends the application through the necessary memory phases and provides a runtime environment to orchestrate execution on the target hardware platform. Our approach requires minimal changes to the application implementation since the deployment tool directly extracts the required information from the generated application binaries. We validate our methodology using an avionics case study on a RISC-V multicore system, showcasing its key components and principles of operation.

</details>


### [106] [Physics-Direct FPGA Tooth-Contact Computation for Deterministic Gear Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04248)
*Jiacheng Miao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"physics-direct"方法：将齿轮接触物理（接触点定位、主曲率提取、椭圆赫兹解）直接压入无分支定点FPGA数据通路，替代依赖数据且耗时的LTCA求解器和缺乏泛化保障的神经网络代理。在开源openXC7流程下实现于XC7K480T，零训练即可在负载外推上优于训练网络，延迟为编译期常数（零抖动）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 齿轮数字孪生、硬件在环台架和主动振动控制要求瞬时齿接触状态的闭环求解，需要实时、确定性、可嵌入的求解器。现有LTCA是数据相关的线性互补问题（LCP），fp64下每个啮合相位需数秒，结构上不具备实时性；学习式代理虽推理快，但每个训练样本需一次完整LTCA求解，且对训练包络之外无任何保证。

Method: 将闭环形式的接触物理（接触点定位、主曲率提取、椭圆赫兹解）直接压缩为无分支定点FPGA数据通路（physics-direct），采用完全开源的openXC7流程（无厂商工具、无浮点IP），在退役的XC7K480T芯片上验证。实现了四个SoC（几何、赫兹、两条融合通路），通过JTAG回读与黄金模型进行逐位精确比对。

Result: 片上预览对LCP体压力跟踪误差在-22%以内，加载传动误差（LTE）峰峰值误差在-19%以内，保持精确的W^{1/3}定律；零训练情况下在负载维度外推比训练网络更准确（18.9%对20.3%）。延迟为编译期常数（σ=0抖动），DSP受限的几何内核在近63条通道上饱和织物资源。

Conclusion: Physics-direct是可解释、确定性的嵌入式齿接触估计方案，可作为神经代理的替代品，兼顾实时性与全程确定性，且具有零训练的负载外推能力。

Abstract: Gear digital twins, hardware-in-the-loop rigs, and active vibration control close a loop around the instantaneous tooth-contact state, demanding a solver that is real-time, deterministic, and embeddable -- properties that loaded tooth contact analysis (LTCA), a data-dependent linear complementarity problem (LCP) costing seconds per mesh phase in fp64, structurally lacks; learned surrogates infer fast but spend one full LTCA solve per training sample and offer no guarantee beyond their training envelope. We instead compress the closed-form contact physics -- contact-point localization, principal-curvature extraction, and the elliptical Hertz solution -- directly into a branch-free fixed-point FPGA datapath, termed physics-direct, realized through the fully open-source openXC7 flow (no vendor tools, no floating-point IP) and validated on retired XC7K480T silicon. Four SoCs -- geometry, Hertz, and two fused paths -- pass bit-exact JTAG readback against a golden model. The on-chip preview tracks LCP body pressure to within -22% and loaded transmission error (LTE) peak-to-peak to -19%, preserving the exact $W^{1/3}$ law; with zero training it extrapolates in load more accurately than a trained network (18.9% versus 20.3%). Latency is a compile-time constant ($σ= 0$ jitter), and the DSP-bound geometry kernel saturates the fabric near 63 lanes. Physics-direct is an interpretable, deterministic alternative to neural surrogacy for embedded tooth-contact estimation.

</details>


### [107] [Reviewer Capability Governs Rejection Targeting, Not Repair Skill: Evidence from LLM Execute-Review-Revise Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04270)
*Faizan Tanveer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对100道奥林匹克数学难题的受控实验，研究多智能体LLM流水线中审查者能力差异对最终结果的影响。研究发现：跨家族中档审查者将最终准确率提升12个百分点（52%到64%），且零伤害；相同模型自审查虽有最高的错误检测率（0.85召回率）却无显著增益，因其过度拒绝且修复率仅为三分之一；低能力审查者低于能力下限后变的惰性。研究为受控试点而非普适结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM流水线通常将执行和验证角色分配给不同能力级别的模型，因为每个阶段都使用旗舰模型成本高昂。以往研究虽已确认验证阶段并非总是有益，但基本保持审查者能力相对于执行者固定不变。本文旨在探究当审查者能力变化（从能解决问题到完全不能解决）时，验证环节究竟如何影响整体结果。

Method: 本文在固定100道奥林匹克数学难题上，保持单一执行者-审查者配置，系统性地将审查者替换为横跨不同能力层级的模型（包括跨家族中档模型、同族同级别模型以及能力最弱以至于完全无法解题的模型），并逐一测量每次拒绝的下游结果（修复率、误拒绝率及最终准确率影响）。

Result: 关键结果：(1) 跨家族中档审查者将最终准确率从52%提升至64%（+12个百分点，p=0.0005），且零错误损伤；(2) 同模型自审查错误检测率最高（0.85召回率）但无显著准确率增益，其拒绝频率是对照的2.1倍，而修复率仅15%对43%（p=0.0074），并误拒绝自身35%的正确解答（对照仅2%，配对p=0.000015）；(3) 自审的低损伤率是"修订惰性"而非审查质量的体现：18个被误拒绝的正确解答中，执行者遵从修订的3个均变错误，而忽略的15个保持不变；(4) 弱审查者低于能力下限后完全惰性，100个最终答案零更改却使token成本翻倍。

Conclusion: 审查者能力与执行者的相对关系对多智能体LLM流水线至关重要：适度异质（跨家族中档能力）的审查者能带来显著且安全的准确率提升，而自审查虽有高错误检测率却因过度拒绝和低修复率而无增益；审查者存在能力下限，低于该下限验证角色将变得完全无效，只增加成本。作者强调此为100道题上的单配置受控试点，不应外推为关于验证环节的普适性结论。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM pipelines increasingly assign roles, including execution and verification, to models of different capability tiers. This is done because running a flagship model at every stage is expensive. Previous literature has established that verification stages are not always beneficial, but holds reviewer capability roughly fixed relative to the executor. We vary it. We replace the reviewer with models spanning a capability range down to one that cannot solve the problems at all, and measure the outcome of every individual rejection. This is done across a constant set of 100 olympiad mathematics problems.
  A cross-family mid-tier reviewer improves final accuracy by 12 percentage points, from 52 to 64 percent (p = 0.0005), with zero damaged answers. Same-model self-review attains the highest error-detection rate of any condition (0.85 recall) yet yields no significant gain: it rejects 2.1 times as often for a third the repair rate (15 against 43 percent, p = 0.0074) and falsely rejects 35 percent of its own correct answers against 2 percent for the cross-family reviewer (paired p = 0.000015). The low damage rate of self-review proves to be an artifact of revision inertia rather than reviewer quality: of 18 falsely rejected correct answers, the three where the executor complied all became wrong, while the fifteen it ignored survived unchanged. Below a capability floor the role becomes inert: our weakest reviewer changed zero of 100 final answers while doubling token cost. These findings describe a single executor-reviewer configuration on 100 problems and should be read as a controlled pilot rather than a general claim about verification stages.

</details>


### [108] [Integrating Crash Report Mining and LLMs for Bug Localization and Repair: An Industrial Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04483)
*Marcos Medeiros,Uirá Kulesza,Christoph Treude,Daniel Lucena,Rafael Gomes,Roberta Coelho,Eiji Adachi,Rodrigo Bonifacio*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了将崩溃报告挖掘技术（堆栈轨迹聚类与可疑文件/方法排序）与大语言模型（LLM）结合，用于工业生产环境中崩溃缺陷的定位与修复。通过回顾性评估5种LLM在4种提示配置下的表现，选出最优模型在来自两个大型Java企业系统的38个崩溃缺陷上进行测试，结果表明最优配置能定位71%的崩溃缺陷并正确修复52%，为工业维护流程中LLM辅助调试提供了实证依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模工业软件系统中崩溃报告缺陷的分析需要大量维护投入，尤其在生产环境中开发者需处理海量崩溃报告和源代码工件来定位和修复根因。虽然已有研究表明LLM可以辅助维护任务，但关于LLM在工业场景下支持崩溃报告缺陷分析和批量修复的有效性知之甚少。

Method: 将崩溃报告挖掘技术（堆栈轨迹聚类和可疑文件与方法排序）与LLM相结合，对5种LLM在4种提示配置下进行回顾性评估，选出最优模型后在来自两个大型Java企业系统的38个崩溃缺陷上运行，并通过手动验证评估LLM生成回答的结构特征、解释模式以及定位和修复的有效性。

Result: 最优配置在完整数据集上能够定位高达71%的崩溃缺陷，并正确修复52%的崩溃缺陷。

Conclusion: 将崩溃报告挖掘与基于LLM的修复相结合，能够有效支持工业维护工作流程中的调试活动，为在实际生产环境中部署此类技术提供了经验证据。

Abstract: Analyzing crash-report bugs in large-scale industrial software systems requires substantial maintenance effort, particularly in production environments where developers must handle large volumes of crash reports and source code artifacts to localize and fix their root causes. While recent studies have shown that Large Language Models (LLMs) can assist with maintenance tasks, little is known about their effectiveness in supporting developers in analyzing crash-report bugs and repairing bugs associated with groups of crash reports in industrial settings. To address this gap, we investigate whether integrating crash report mining techniques---specifically stack trace clustering and suspicious file and method ranking---with LLMs can support crash localization and repair in production environments. We conduct a retrospective evaluation of five LLMs under four prompt configurations. After that, we chose the best model to run on 38 crash bugs collected from two large Java enterprise systems. We further analyze the structural characteristics and explanatory patterns of LLM-generated responses and assess localization and repair effectiveness through manual validation. Our results show that the best-performing configuration localizes up to 71% and correctly repairs 52% of crash bugs on the full dataset. These findings provide empirical evidence that combining crash report mining with LLM-based repair can effectively support debugging activities in industrial maintenance workflows.

</details>


### [109] [An Empirical Analysis of CodeQL False Positives and Query Refinements for Java Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04535)
*Amirali Sajadi,Saikat Dutta,Preetha Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究CodeQL在Java安全分析中的误报问题，通过手动审查500个误报样本构建了源码级分类体系（五大类误报模式），并基于此实现CodeQL查询级精化来消除误报。固定精化可移除81.8%的已审查误报，但存在项目特异性泛化瓶颈；将精化模式作为模板交给Agentic编码工具后，成功率从28%提升至56%-62%，证明了"精化导向的SAST工作流"的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAST工具（如CodeQL）在漏洞发现上虽有帮助，但大量误报给开发者带来了巨大的分类审查负担。作者希望通过系统性地研究误报是否形成可重复、可解释的模式，并通过精化分析来减少误报，降低人工分类工作。

Method: (1) 在110个项目的167个CVE实例上运行CodeQL Java安全查询套件，聚焦10个误报率最高的查询；(2) 手动审查500条误报路径和位置，构建包含5类误报的源码级分类体系（遗漏路径约束/净化、良性执行上下文、缺失信任边界建模、并发建模不精确、Sink建模不精确）；(3) 基于分类发现实现CodeQL查询级精化来过滤重复误报模式；(4) 进一步评估Agentic编码工具能否自动将精化模式适配到新项目。

Result: 五类误报分类占比分别为36.6%、29.4%、27.6%、5%、1.4%。固定精化移除了81.8%的已审查误报；在整个选定查询数据集中移除15.8%的路径和位置，同时保留8个真阳性中的7个。Agentic工具在提供精化模式作为模板时成功率达56%和62%（编译通过率>90%），无引导时仅为28%（编译率30-36%）。

Conclusion: CodeQL Java安全分析中的误报确实形成可重复、可解释的模式，且可通过对查询进行精化来系统性减少。虽然固定精化依赖项目特定上下文，但Agentic编码工具能够以模板方式将精化模式自动适配到新项目，支持一种"精化导向的SAST工作流"，从而减少重复性人工分类工作。

Abstract: Static application security testing (SAST) tools help developers find vulnerabilities before deployment, but false positives create substantial triage effort. We study whether CodeQL false positives in Java security analysis form recurring, explainable patterns that can be reduced by refining the analysis. We run CodeQL's Java security query suite on 167 CVE instances from 110 projects, focusing on the ten queries with the highest false positive rates. We manually review 500 sampled false positive paths and locations and construct a source-level taxonomy. The five categories are Missed Path Constraint or Sanitization (36.6%), Benign Execution Context (29.4%), Missing Trust Boundary Modeling (27.6%), Imprecise Concurrency Modeling (5%), and Imprecise Sink Modeling (1.4%).
  Guided by these findings, we implement CodeQL refinements that detect and filter recurring false positive patterns at the query level. The refinements remove 81.8% of reviewed false positives. Across the full selected-query dataset, they remove 15.8% of reported paths and locations while retaining 7 of 8 true positives. This shows that many false positives can be reduced in the analysis, although fixed refinements often depend on project-specific context. To address this generalization gap, we evaluate whether agentic coding tools can adapt refinement patterns to new projects. Given our patterns as templates, the two tools succeed on 56% and 62% of tasks, with query compile-pass rates above 90%. Without this guidance, both succeed on only 28%, while compile rates fall to 30-36%. These results support a refinement-oriented SAST workflow in which recurring false positives are modeled in CodeQL queries and automatically adapted to different project contexts, reducing repeated triage.

</details>


### [110] [Dynamic Adaptation of the LLM Context for Generating Routines with Coupled Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04570)
*Gnaneswar Villuri,Hashmath Shaik,Alex Doboli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出动态上下文自适应（dynamic context adaptation）方法，用于解决LLM代码生成中依赖执行期耦合（execution-dependent coupling）的正确性问题。通过验证体从执行轨迹提取结构化诊断信息作为类梯度指导、知识图谱提供语义约束、模拟退火选择候选，在300和600次评估下显著超越Zero-shot、Reflexion和OpenEvolve等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成在面对"静态绑定"（static binding）问题时失效——即一个例程的含义由另一例程的运行时行为定义，仅凭文本描述无法解析这种关系。这种限制不止出现在显式耦合问题中，在打包、路由、符号搜索等涉及跨组件联合执行约束的问题中也不同程度存在，需要一种针对该场景的样本高效的验证-生成循环机制。

Method: 提出动态上下文自适应（dynamic context adaptation）框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 验证代理从执行轨迹中提取结构化诊断信息，为生成代理提供类似梯度的引导；(2) 由问题描述构建的知识图谱向生成代理提供语义约束；(3) 采用模拟退火在候选中进行选择以避免贪婪坍塌。每轮迭代生成多个候选方案。

Result: 在八个问题中的七个上，300次和600次评估时均显著优于Zero-shot、Reflexion和OpenEvolve（p<0.01），处于基于种群搜索尚未积累足够多样性的评估预算区间。在主要动机问题（交叉耦合优化）上，该方法在1000次评估时也取得最佳成绩，支持结构化执行反馈在依赖运行时耦合场景下最有效的假设。消融实验确认结构化执行反馈是主要驱动力。

Conclusion: 结构化执行反馈在依赖运行时耦合的代码生成任务中具有关键价值，动态上下文自适应方法在低评估预算下即可展现优势，且验证-生成循环中的梯度式诊断指导是性能提升的核心来源。

Abstract: LLM-based code generation fails when correctness depends on execution-dependent coupling: the meaning of one routine is defined by the runtime behavior of another, a relationship that cannot be resolved from textual descriptions alone. This limitation, which we call static binding, is not confined to explicitly coupled problems; it appears to varying degrees whenever correctness depends on joint execution behavior across components, from explicit cross-coupled optimizers to subtler joint constraints in packing, routing, and symbolic search. This paper proposes dynamic context adaptation, a sample-efficient validation-generation loop designed for this setting. A validation agent extracts structured diagnostic information from execution traces, providing gradient-like guidance to a generation agent that proposes multiple candidates per iteration. A knowledge graph derived from the problem description supplies semantic constraints to the generation agent. Simulated annealing selects among candidates to avoid greedy collapse. Our method outperforms zero-shot, Reflexion, and OpenEvolve on seven of eight problems at both 300 and 600 evaluations (p < 0.01), a regime where population-based search has not yet accumulated sufficient diversity to compete. Notably, on the primary motivating problem (cross-coupled optimization), our method also achieves the best score at 1000 evaluations, consistent with the hypothesis that structured execution feedback is most beneficial when correctness depends on runtime coupling. Ablation results confirm that structured execution feedback is the primary driver.

</details>


### [111] [Software Engineering in the Agent Era From Trustworthy Change to Human Agent Software Organizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04630)
*Zhongjie Wang,Mingyi Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可测试的治理框架，用于管理软件代理带来的可扩展执行能力。该框架包含两个构念（可信变更TC和责任拓扑）及一个执行抽象（人-代理单元HAC），旨在回答"当数字执行可规模化复制和并行化时，组织应如何治理变更以接受并维持其影响"这一核心问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件代理使数字执行具有弹性，仓库分析、代码生成、测试、迁移、工具使用和运维均可无需成比例增加人力而复制和并行化。但问题定义、语义承诺、验证、集成、注意力及残余风险接受仍受人类认知、组织权威和经济能力的约束。现有的责任、问责、变更管理等概念虽早已存在，但在代理规模化执行背景下它们需要重新组合，因此需要一个显式的治理框架来说明权威分配如何与执行能力同步演化。

Method: 提出理论框架，包含：(1) Trustworthy Change（可信变更）作为核心工程对象，描述从意图经委派执行、验证、集成、接受到运维的完整流转；(2) Responsibility Topology（责任拓扑）按独立残余风险接受权威的分布对组织分类：单中心拓扑有一个最终基线责任锚点，多锚点拓扑要求跨独立治理域的联合接受；(3) Human-Agent Cell（人-代理单元）作为执行抽象，产生候选方案、提案和证据，但执行本身不授予任何接受权威。框架进一步推导了权威分类轴对变更状态、共享工程事实、验证和流程控制的后果。

Result: 本文贡献理论建构与操作化：指出代理规模化执行改变的是既有概念（责任、问责、变更管理、规范、验证、人工监督）的"组合方式"而非取代它们；将权威作为显式分类轴，推导出分布式人-代理单元在上下文连贯性和失效压力方面的治理后果，以及多锚点治理下的联合接受和显式责任闭合。渐进式规范和有限容量分析仅作为待检验假设提出，经验有效性留待对照、纵向和现场研究验证。

Conclusion: 治理的核心挑战在于执行能力与权威能力规模化速率的不一致：当执行可无限制扩展时，残余风险接受的权威边界必须被显式化。单中心拓扑提供基线明确但可能成为瓶颈，多锚点拓扑强调联合接受和责任闭合。该框架为组织和研究者提供了一套可操作的概念工具来审视代理化软件开发中的治理问题，但其经验效度仍需实证检验。

Abstract: Software agents make digital execution elastic: repository analysis, code generation, testing, migration, tool use, and operations can be replicated and parallelized without proportional human headcount. Problem framing, semantic commitment, verification, integration, attention, and residual-risk acceptance remain bounded by human cognition, organizational authority, and economic capacity. How should scalable execution be governed so organizations can accept and sustain its changes?
  Our testable framework has two constructs and one execution abstraction. Trustworthy Change (TC) is the engineering object moving from intent through delegated execution, verification, integration, acceptance, and operation. Responsibility Topology classifies organizations by the distribution of independent residual-risk acceptance authority. A single-center topology has one final baseline responsibility anchor; a multi-anchor topology requires joint acceptance across independently governed domains. The Human-Agent Cell (HAC) produces candidates, proposals, and evidence; execution grants no acceptance authority. As execution and authority scale differently, distributed HACs create context-coherence and invalidation pressures, while multi-anchor governance adds joint acceptance and explicit responsibility closure.
  Responsibility, accountability, change management, specification, verification, and human oversight predate this work; our claim is only that agent-scaled execution changes how they fit together. We make that authority an explicit classification axis and derive consequences for change state, shared engineering facts, verification, and flow control. Progressive Specification and bounded-capacity analysis remain hypotheses to test, not laws. We contribute theory construction and operationalization; empirical validity remains open to controlled, longitudinal, and field studies.

</details>


### [112] [How Developers Discuss Generative AI: A Longitudinal Study of the Visual Studio Code Community](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04680)
*Panida Rumriankit,Akito Monden,Hiroki Inayoshi,Pattara Leelaprute,Bundit Manaskasemsak,Kundjanasith Thonglek,Arnon Rungsawang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对VS Code GitHub仓库中2021年1月至2026年6月间的43,806个候选issue进行纵向分析，经过关键词检索与语义相关性过滤后获得25,227个AI相关issue，采用BERTopic进行主题识别，并用月度流行度和Mann-Kendall趋势检验分析主题的时间演变。研究发现开发者讨论主要集中在AI辅助开发环境的操作层面（如代理管理、配置、可靠性、认证和计费），而调查研究中强调的风险（如幻觉和许可）很少出现；讨论主题从AI辅助代码补全转向对话式和基于代理的开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具已深度融入日常软件开发实践，但主流开源社区如何在实际中讨论这些工具仍缺乏系统性的实证理解，需要填补这一研究空白。

Method: 采用纵向分析方法：首先结合关键词检索与语义相关性过滤构建高质量的语料库；其次应用BERTopic识别讨论主题，并使用过滤后语料进行主题验证和稳健性重新聚类；最后通过月度流行度和Mann-Kendall趋势检验分析主题随时间演化的规律。

Result: 开发者讨论以AI辅助开发环境的实际操作关切为主（代理管理、配置、可靠性、认证、计费），而调查型研究中强调的风险话题（幻觉、许可）在该平台上鲜有出现；讨论主题从AI辅助代码补全演进为对话式和基于代理的开发模式。

Conclusion: GitHub Issues提供了以工作流为导向的生成式AI实践视角，与基于调查的开发者感知研究形成互补；VS Code issue追踪器中的相关讨论主要聚焦于AI辅助软件开发的运维层面。

Abstract: Generative AI tools such as GitHub Copilot, ChatGPT, and coding agents have rapidly become part of everyday software development, yet little is known about how mainstream open source communities discuss them in practice. This paper presents a longitudinal analysis of generative-AI-related discussions in the Visual Studio Code (VS Code) GitHub repository, using 43,806 candidate issues created between January 2021 and June 2026. To improve corpus quality, we combined keyword retrieval with semantic relevance filtering, yielding a filtered corpus of 25,227 AI-related issues. We applied BERTopic to the retrieved corpus to identify discussion topics, using the filtered corpus for theme validation and a robustness re-clustering, and analyzed their evolution over time using monthly prevalence and Mann-Kendall trend tests. The results show that developer discussions are dominated by practical concerns regarding the operation of AI-assisted development environments, including agent management, configuration, reliability, authentication, and billing, whereas risks frequently emphasized in survey-based studies, such as hallucination and licensing, rarely surface in this venue. This suggests that discussions of generative AI in the VS Code issue tracker primarily focus on operational aspects of AI-assisted software development. Furthermore, discussions evolved from AI-assisted code completion toward conversational and agent-based development, reflecting the increasing integration of generative AI into software development workflows. These findings suggest that GitHub Issues provide a practical, workflow-oriented perspective on generative AI that complements survey-based studies of developer perceptions.

</details>


### [113] [Beyond Code Generation: Reliability, Verification, and Cost Economics in the Agentic Software Development Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04681)
*Happy Bhati*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI编码系统从代码补全/聊天演进为可自主检查仓库、编辑文件、运行工具、编写测试并长时间无监督工作的智能体后，软件交付瓶颈的转变。通过综合2024至2026年间的同行评审研究、基准审计、生产报告等证据，提出四个核心工程概念（智能体SDLC吞吐悖论、生产合格变更PQC、验证税、智能体SDLC控制平面），将研究问题从"智能体能生成多少代码"转向"工程系统每美元、每审阅小时、每单位运营风险能交付多少生产合格价值"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程系统正从自动补全和聊天工具演变为能在仓库中自主工作的智能体，虽显著提升了编码活动效率，但近期证据表明这些增益在从写代码到交付可靠软件之间存在急剧衰减。评审、集成、测试、安全、部署和生产运维仍是瓶颈环节，同时成本模式从可预测的按席位许可转向可变的token、工具、沙箱、CI和返工成本。作者旨在从工程经济学角度系统性地重新定义智能体时代的核心问题。

Method: 本文不提出新的模型实验，而是对2024年至2026年9月间的多种来源证据进行综合提炼，包括：同行评审的软件工程研究、大学研究、基准审计、大型科技公司的生产报告、开发者遥测数据及成本管理证据。在此基础上提出四个概念性工程框架：Agentic SDLC吞吐悖论、生产合格变更(PQC)、验证税和智能体SDLC控制平面，并构建了从当前受监督智能体到未来策略约束的软件工厂的基于证据的演进路线图。

Result: 该研究识别出智能体编码能力提升与软件实际交付之间的显著失衡：上游编码活动增益明显，但下游的评审、集成、测试等环节成为新的约束瓶颈。经济层面呈现从固定许可成本向可变的token、工具、沙盒和返工成本的转变。四个概念框架的提出为理解和管理智能体时代软件交付的效率与风险提供了结构化视角。

Conclusion: 智能体软件工程的核心研究问题应从"智能体能生成多少代码"转变为"工程系统每单位货币成本、每审阅人小时及每单位运营风险能交付多少生产合格价值"。未来的发展路径是从当前受监督的智能体演进到受策略约束的软件工厂，需要建立能够根据成本、可靠性和人类注意力预算来分配自主权的控制平面。

Abstract: AI coding systems are moving from autocomplete and chat toward agents that can inspect repositories, edit multiple files, run tools, write tests, open pull requests, and work for long periods with limited supervision. This capability changes the bottleneck in software delivery. Recent field studies show meaningful gains in coding activity, but newer evidence also shows that those gains attenuate sharply between writing code and shipping reliable software. Review, integration, testing, security, deployment, and production operations remain constraining stages, while the economics are shifting from predictable per-seat licensing toward variable token, tool, sandbox, CI, and rework costs.
  This paper synthesizes peer-reviewed software-engineering research, university studies, benchmark audits, production reports from major technology companies, developer telemetry, and cost-management evidence released primarily from 2024 through September 2026. No new model experiment is claimed; numerical findings remain attributed to their original studies. The synthesis proposes four engineering concepts: the Agentic SDLC Throughput Paradox, Production-Qualified Change (PQC), the Verification Tax, and an Agentic SDLC Control Plane that allocates autonomy subject to cost, reliability, and human-attention budgets. An evidence-based horizon then maps today's supervised agents to future policy-bounded software factories. The central research question shifts from how much code an agent can generate to how much production-qualified value an engineering system can deliver per dollar, per reviewer-hour, and per unit of operational risk.

</details>


### [114] [Building a research-software catalog with a coding agent: from hackathon prototype to public deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04711)
*Kazuyoshi Yoshimi,Satoshi Terasaki,Gotai Yamada*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了在三天黑客松期间开发的研究软件仓库目录原型，以及为公开部署所需的工程化改造经验（包括对抗性审查、数据质量检查、浏览器级验证和发布保障）。作者进一步将这些经验迁移到更大规模的人工策展门户MateriApps的检索代理开发中，并发现AI辅助软件门户面临的主要问题不是崩溃，而是产生看似合理但不完整或错误输出的静默失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI和编码代理加速了研究软件的开发，但也增加了对软件高效发现与维护的需求。作者希望探索如何构建和维护面向研究软件的门户目录，并理解AI辅助工具在该场景中的实际可靠性与局限性。

Method: 在一个三天黑客松中开发了仓库目录原型，随后进行公开部署所需的工程化改造，包括对抗性审查、数据质量检查、浏览器级验证和发布保障；再将原型经验迁移到MateriApps门户的检索代理（结合策展门户元数据、外部文档、向量搜索与本地语言模型生成）开发中，并进行对比分析。

Result: 编码代理实现速度快，但达到可靠运行需要大量额外工程。最严重的问题并非系统崩溃，而是静默失败——输出看似合理却不完整或错误，根因包括数据采集不完整、误导性评估以及检索或预处理失败。MateriApps的探索性研究仍处于活跃开发中，其观察结论为初步结果。

Conclusion: AI辅助软件门户需要明确的验证、监控和重复审查机制；策展元数据和维护良好的文档仍然不可或缺。结合策展元数据、自动收集文档和基于检索的辅助方案，可能对扩展其他研究软件门户具有借鉴价值。

Abstract: Generative AI and coding agents can accelerate research software development, but they also increase the need for efficient software discovery and maintenance. We developed a repository catalog during a three-day hackathon and subsequently examined the engineering required to make it suitable for public deployment, including adversarial review, data-quality checks, browser-level validation, and publication safeguards. We then explored whether the lessons learned from this prototype could be transferred to a much larger, human-curated portal, through a retrieval agent under development for MateriApps that combines curated portal metadata, external documentation, vector search, and local language-model generation. Implementation with coding agents was rapid, but achieving reliable operation required substantial additional engineering: the most consequential problems were not crashes but silent failures that produced plausible yet incomplete or incorrect outputs, arising from incomplete data acquisition, misleading assessments, and retrieval or preprocessing failures. These observations suggest that AI-assisted software portals require explicit validation, monitoring, and repeated review, and that curated metadata and maintained documentation remain essential. The MateriApps work is exploratory and remains under active development, so the observations reported for it are preliminary; a comparable combination of curated metadata, automatically collected documentation, and retrieval-based assistance may nevertheless be useful for extending other research-software portals.

</details>


### [115] [Same Request, Different Answer: Quantization Amplifies Cache-Induced Divergence in LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04748)
*Aditi Patodiya*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了主流开源推理引擎中默认启用的前缀缓存（prefix caching）对可复现性的影响，发现启用缓存后，权重量化程度越高，结果偏差越大：在4-bit量化下75%的agentic tool-use工作流轨迹被改变，而在禁用缓存时所有配置下均可实现比特级可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前缀缓存作为推理引擎的默认优化，被视为透明无害，但本研究质疑其对可复现性的实际代价。在agentic工作流、科学研究等需要复现结果的场景中，不可复现性会导致严重问题。作者发现这种代价随着权重量化而急剧增加，因此亟需系统量化其影响并定位根源。

Method: 设计了一个80集的多轮agentic tool-use工作负载，在固定模型、解码参数、种子和请求顺序及batch size=1的串行条件下，跨两个推理引擎和四种权重格式（16-bit至4-bit），分别开启和关闭缓存进行对照实验。随后通过受控缓存配置、改变服务器级prompt-cache设置、调整执行顺序和恢复缓存状态等三个实验定位分岐根源，并用单轮推理桥接实验验证其任务级影响。

Result: 启用缓存使agent轨迹在16-bit精度下改变36.2%，在4-bit精度下改变75.0%。禁用缓存时800次episode全部比特级一致，将其他非确定性因素界定在0.5%以内。服务器级prompt-cache设置可移动run-to-run分岐37.5个百分点，执行顺序仅在设置生效时起作用，恢复缓存状态后cached与recompute路径各自在40/40项上可复现但仍存在14项彼此的差异。单轮实验表明分岐可影响任务结果但汇总准确率不变。

Conclusion: 带缓存的推理在缓存状态确定时是确定性的，实际中不可复现的根源在于缓存状态未包含在请求中且默认从不重置。研究揭示了前缀缓存与量化叠加带来的隐性可复现性风险，并发布了可复用的复现工具链。

Abstract: Prefix caching, in which a serving engine reuses the key and value tensors of a shared prompt prefix across requests, is enabled by default in the major open-source stacks and treated as a transparent optimization. We measure what it costs in reproducibility, and find that the cost rises sharply with weight quantization. Holding the model, decoding parameters, seed, and request order fixed, and issuing every request serially at batch size one, we ran an eighty-episode multi-turn agentic tool-use workload with caching enabled and disabled across two engines and four weight formats. Enabling the cache changed the agent's trajectory on 36.2 percent of episodes at 16-bit precision and on 75.0 percent at four-bit, a gradient that survives re-measurement under a controlled cache configuration. With caching disabled, repeated execution was bit-identical in every configuration, 0 of 800 episodes, which bounds other sources of nondeterminism at 0.5 percent. Repeated cache-enabled runs did diverge, and three experiments locate the cause: a single server-level prompt-cache setting moves run-to-run divergence by 37.5 percentage points, execution order acts only while that setting is active, and restoring cache state makes the cached and recompute paths each reproduce on 40 of 40 items while still differing from each other on 14. Cached serving is deterministic given cache state, and irreproducible in practice because that state is absent from the request and never reset by default. A single-turn bridge shows the divergence reaching task outcomes without shifting aggregate accuracy. We release the harness, logs, and analysis pipeline.

</details>


### [116] [Better Understanding, Better Fixes? A Study of Hallucination in LLM-based Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04909)
*Xuemeng Cai,Jiakun Liu,Linhan Yang,Wei Ma,Lingxiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对LLM在自动程序修复（APR）过程中的幻觉现象进行了多层次分析，将幻觉划分为最终补丁中的修复幻觉和中间产物中的理解幻觉，通过三个任务（触发测试用例识别、行覆盖率预测、额外测试用例生成）在832个Defects4J缺陷上评估了三个代表性LLM，发现修复幻觉和理解幻觉普遍存在，且中间产物的准确性虽与修复成功相关但并非严格保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的自动程序修复评估主要关注最终结果（result-centric），缺乏对修复过程中幻觉现象的系统性分析。幻觉不仅可能出现在最终补丁中，也可能出现在引导补丁生成的中间产物中，而这一问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: 将幻觉定义为生成的补丁或中间产物未忠实基于可用修复证据。通过三个任务（触发测试用例识别、行覆盖率预测、额外测试用例生成）考察中间产物中的理解幻觉，并对最终补丁中的修复幻觉进行考察。在832个Defects4J缺陷上，结合自动评估和人工分析，对三个代表性LLM进行评估。

Result: 各模型和设置下仅有21.0%-55.9%的生成补丁能通过开发者编写的测试套件。人工分析812个修复样本发现72.7%存在修复幻觉（包括通过所有可用测试的补丁），其中错误的因果定位占45.9%，错误修复策略占18.5%。模型在触发测试用例识别、涉及分支控制流的行覆盖率预测以及额外测试用例生成方面经常出错。中间产物更准确与修复成功相关，但此关系并非始终成立。

Conclusion: LLM在程序修复中的幻觉问题严重且多层次，不仅存在于最终补丁，还广泛存在于中间理解产物中。当前方法对修复证据的利用不足，需要更系统的方法来减少修复过程中的幻觉。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have significantly advanced automated program repair (APR), yet existing evaluations remain largely result-centric and provide limited insight into hallucination during repair. In APR, hallucination may arise not only in final patches but also in the intermediate artifacts that guide patch generation. To address this gap, we perform a multi-layered analysis of hallucination throughout the APR process. Specifically, we characterize hallucination as the production of patches or intermediate artifacts that are not faithfully grounded in the available repair evidence. We examine repair hallucination in final patches and understanding hallucination in intermediate artifacts through three tasks, namely triggering testcase identification, line coverage prediction, and additional testcase generation.We then evaluate three representative LLMs on 832 Defects4J bugs through automatic evaluation and manual analysis. Our results show that both repair and understanding hallucinations remain prevalent. Across models and settings, only 21.0%-55.9% of generated patches pass the developer-written test suite. Moreover, although more accurate intermediate artifacts are generally associated with successful repairs, this relationship does not always hold. Manual analysis of 812 sampled repairs identifies repair hallucinations in 72.7% of cases, including patches that pass all available tests; incorrect causal localization and incorrect repair strategies account for 45.9% and 18.5% of these hallucinations, respectively. Meanwhile, models frequently misidentify triggering testcases, mispredict line coverage involving branching control flow, and generate additional testcases with missing bug-triggering conditions or incorrect expected behavior.

</details>


### [117] [ARIA - An Agentic Framework for Autonomous Testing of Infotainment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04913)
*António Azevedo,Bruno Lima,João Pascoal Faria*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ARIA是一个多智能体LLM框架，通过视觉交互在Android车载信息娱乐系统上自主运行端到端测试。它采用每个步骤四个专用智能体的闭环流水线加报告阶段，在30个场景中完成28个（93.3%），20个匹配ground truth（71.4%），捕获了全部5个已知缺陷，无误报漏检。相比单智能体基线的72.0%误报率，多智能体设计将误报率降至52.6%，验证了其在工业车载系统测试中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载信息娱乐系统验证仍依赖手动测试，效率低、成本高，且不适应敏捷发布和OTA更新。现有脚本化自动化将测试逻辑与实现耦合，导致脆弱且维护成本高。已有的LLM驱动框架多针对Web/移动应用，采用单或双智能体设置，将感知、规划、动作选择和验证过度集中在少数模型上，在信息娱乐系统的复杂性下易产生幻觉和无效率的探索循环。

Method: ARIA采用多智能体LLM架构，通过视觉交互在Android信息娱乐系统上自主运行端到端测试。其核心是每个步骤使用四个专用智能体的闭环流水线，外加一个报告阶段。每个智能体分别负责感知、规划、动作选择和验证，从单句场景描述（路径、动作、预期结果）出发，执行交互并生成报告、可复现脚本及每步的视觉证据。

Result: 在制造商物理Android信息娱乐系统上，跨30个场景评估：ARIA完成28个场景（93.3%）并给出判定（2个出错），其中20个（71.4%）匹配ground truth。系统捕获全部5个已知缺陷，无误报漏检；8个误报源于导航/图像限制及不支持的姿势。单智能体基线首次通过时误报率高达72.0%（对比ARIA的52.6%），证明多智能体设计的优势。论文报告了首次通过/再次复查结果、每场景的token/调用/成本数据，并通过重复运行证明稳定性随复杂度变化，缺陷检测完全一致。

Conclusion: ARIA证明多智能体LLM框架能够工业级地在车载信息娱乐系统上运行视觉测试，但同时揭示了低误报容错所带来的成本。缺陷检测的完全一致性表明该方法有潜力集成到CI流水线中实现视觉测试的持续集成。单智能体基线对比进一步确认了多智能体设计在降低误报、区分导航困难与系统故障方面的价值。

Abstract: Automotive infotainment validation still relies on manual testing, slow, costly, and incompatible with agile releases and OTA updates. Scripted automation only partly helps: it couples test logic to implementation, yielding brittle, high-maintenance suites. Existing LLM-driven frameworks mostly target web/mobile apps, using single- or dual-agent setups that overload one or two models with perception, planning, action selection, and validation at once, prone to hallucinations and unproductive exploration loops given infotainment complexity. We present ARIA (Autonomous Real-time Infotainment Assessment), a multi-agent LLM framework that autonomously runs end-to-end tests on Android infotainment systems via visual interaction, using a closed-loop pipeline of four specialized agents per step plus a report stage. From single-sentence scenarios (path, action, expected outcome), ARIA runs the interactions and produces reports, reproducible scripts, and visual evidence per step. Evaluated on a manufacturer's physical Android infotainment system across 30 scenarios, ARIA completed 28 (93.3%) with a verdict (2 errored), 20 of which (71.4%) matched ground truth. It caught all 5 known defects, no fault passed as working; its 8 false positives stem from navigation/image limits and unsupported gestures, showing multi-agent LLMs can run infotainment tests industrially while exposing the cost of a low false-positive tolerance. A single-agent baseline confirms the multi-agent design's value: on the first pass, before stronger-model revisitation narrows the gap, it shows a far higher false-positive rate (72.0% vs. 52.6%), conflating navigational difficulty with system failure. We report first-pass/post-revisitation results, token/call/cost per scenario, and show via repeated runs that stability tracks complexity, with fault detection perfectly consistent, pointing to CI integration of visual testing.

</details>


### [118] [An Empirical Study on Learning Paths and Gender Dynamics in Scrum Master Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05186)
*Manuela Petrescu,Paul Razvan Petrescu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究聚焦于Scrum Master角色在多元文化软件公司中的学习路径，通过两阶段定性访谈研究发现：Scrum Master的需求取决于团队规模（约六人上限），目前缺乏标准化的软技能学习流程和度量标准，软技能培养多依赖员工自主学习行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Scrum Master角色需求持续增长，但多元文化软件公司中该角色的学习路径、与团队规模的关系、软技能发展监控方式等缺乏系统性研究，需要深入调查以指导职业发展和企业人才管理。

Method: 研究分两个阶段进行，包括两项定性调查（访谈研究），并对结果进行了定性和定量数据分析相结合的处理。

Result: 研究识别出Scrum Master的需求取决于团队规模（六人上限）；不存在标准化的软技能学习流程或进度度量指标；部分公司通过客户反馈、团队反馈或两者结合来评估软技能；许多SM软技能学习举措由员工自主驱动。

Conclusion: 多元文化软件公司中Scrum Master职业路径缺乏系统性的软技能培训标准化方案，企业应针对团队规模差异和软技能评估建立更完善的机制。

Abstract: Context: Agile development methodology has been widely adopted by industry and the demand for experienced professionals in Agile-related roles is persistently high. Objectives: We focus on the learning path for a Scrum Master role in multicultural software companies and investigate the role in relation to team size, together with the learning process for a career path, and how companies monitor soft skills development. Method: We conducted our study in two phases, two qualitative surveys (interview studies) and performed a qualitative and quantitative data analysis of the results. Conclusions: Our results identified that the need for a Scrum Master (SM) depends on the size of the team, with our study indicating a six-member limit. There is no overall standardized process for soft skills learning or metrics to measure progress. Some companies measure soft skills based on feedback received from the client or from the team, and other companies are taking both types of feedback into consideration. Many learning initiatives, especially on soft skills for an SM role, were based on actions of the employees.
  The version of Record of this contribution is published in Software Engineering and Advanced Applications. SEAA 2025. Lecture Notes in Computer Science, vol 16083. Springer, Cham. and available online at: check DOI.

</details>


### [119] [T(r)opical Islands: Visualizing & Understanding Socio-Technical Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05254)
*Adam Štěpánek,Marco Raglianti,Vít Rusňák,Jan Byška,Barbora Kozlíková,Michele Lanza*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种3D可视化方法，将开源项目中的非代码社会技术工件（如缺陷报告、功能请求、论坛帖子等）映射为植被覆盖的岛屿和树木，以直观展示开发主题随时间的变化与演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GitHub等协作开发平台上的项目包含大量非代码社会技术工件（缺陷报告、功能请求、论坛帖子等），它们分散在多个通信渠道且为自然语言形式，导致分析困难、信息碎片化且噪音大。

Method: 提出基于主题岛屿的3D可视化方法：利用机器学习技术对工件进行聚类并提取主题，将主题映射为海面上升起/下沉的植被岛屿，个体工件用不同类型的树木表示，实现从数据挖掘到交互式渲染的完整可视化管线。

Result: 通过多个案例研究验证了该方法能够揭示项目历史中开发主题讨论的洞察；开展了N=34的用户研究，进一步验证了该方法在帮助理解社会技术工件及其演化方面的适用性。

Conclusion: 该3D可视化方法能有效帮助用户理解开源项目中分散的社会技术工件及其随时间演化，主题岛屿的开沉动态直观反映了讨论热度的变化。

Abstract: Projects hosted on collaborative software development platforms, such as GitHub, include many non-code artifacts documenting the project's lifecycle, with its challenges, plans, design, and even community. These socio-technical artifacts include, for example, bug reports, feature requests, and forum posts, offering a useful prospect on the project's evolution. However, these artifacts are dispersed over multiple communication channels and written in natural language, making their analysis difficult, as they are fragmented and with considerable noise.
  We present a 3D visualization approach mapping topics found across a project's socio-technical artifacts onto vegetation-covered islands, where the individual artifacts are depicted as trees of various types. The topic islands rise out of the ocean as they become discussed, to sink again when they are no longer so. We built a prototype implementing the entire visualization pipeline, from data mining to interactive rendering, leveraging machine learning techniques to cluster the artifacts and extract their topics. We present, through several case studies, the insights that our approach elicits about discussions of development topics throughout a project's history. The user study we conducted (N=34) further strengthens our conclusions about its suitability for understanding socio-technical artifacts and their evolution.

</details>


### [120] [Ritgard: T(r)opical Islands of Socio-Technical Artifacts on GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05278)
*Adam Štěpánek,Marco Raglianti,Jan Byška,Barbora Kozlíková,Michele Lanza*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Ritgard，一个挖掘GitHub仓库中社交技术工件（STAs，如Issues、Pull Requests和Discussions）并将其可视化为3D岛屿的工具。每个树代表一个工件，每个岛屿代表通过文本嵌入和摘要提取的主题，岛屿地形随主题活跃度升降以展示项目特征和关注点的演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件项目的非代码工件包含源代码无法提供的架构决策和演进信息，但这些信息分散在不同的通信渠道中且以自然语言书写，难以被轻松提取和探索。作者旨在解决STAs碎片化和难以可视化探索的问题。

Method: Ritgard从GitHub仓库挖掘STAs（Issues、Pull Requests、Discussions），通过文本嵌入与文本摘要的组合提取主题，将每个工件表示为一棵树、每个主题表示为一个岛屿，并以3D可视化方式展示。岛屿地形随主题活跃度升降反映项目特征的演化过程。

Result: 论文描述了Ritgard工具的使用方式和实现细节，展示了其可视化背后的诸多技术挑战。

Conclusion: Ritgard通过创新的3D可视化方法，使开发者能够直观地探索和分析软件项目中分散的社交技术工件，从而获取源代码之外关于架构决策和演化的重要信息。

Abstract: A software project is more than just code. Non-code artifacts often document the human processes and decisions behind source code. The rationale behind a library change, an architectural decision, a problem encountered by a user are all examples of information typically present in socio-technical artifacts (STAs), created and persisted in channels separate from the repository itself (yet sometimes very close---e.g., GitHub Issues with GitHub repositories). These STAs are a trove of information about the project's architecture and its evolution, containing details and insights that code alone cannot provide. Unfortunately, this information is not easily extracted and explored as STAs are frequently fragmented over different communication channels, and are written in natural language.
  We present Ritgard, a tool that mines GitHub repositories for their STAs, namely Issues, Pull Requests, and Discussions, and visualizes them as 3D islands covered with trees. Each tree represents a single artifact and each island is a topic extracted from the artifacts through a combination of text embedding and text summarization. The terrain of the islands rises out of the ocean as the topic becomes active and sinks back in when it becomes stale, thus depicting the evolution of features and concerns throughout the project's lifetime. We describe the tool's usage and implementation, showing the numerous technical challenges behind Ritgard's visualization.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [EXAONE Forecast for Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04239)
*Seunghan Lee,Jaehoon Lee,Jun Seo,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Minjae Kim,Sungdong Yoo,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了EXAONE Forecast for Finance（EXAONE Finance），一个专为金融时间序列预测设计的基础模型。该模型采用无注意力架构（因果1D卷积+分组感知池化MLP替代自注意力），通过掩码上下文增强处理金融数据中普遍的缺失值问题，并在包含股票、外汇、大宗商品、加密资产、固定收益和宏观指标的大规模金融语料上预训练。在FinVerse基准上，该模型在点预测精度、截面资产排序和组合盈利性三个评估层级均取得最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用时间序列基础模型存在三大局限：1）自注意力后端的计算成本随序列长度和变量数量呈二次增长，难以应对金融领域长序列、多信道的数据；2）假设输入完全观测，无法处理金融市场中普遍存在的数据缺失；3）预训练语料未捕捉金融市场的独特动态。这些因素严重制约了通用TSFM在金融场景中的应用。

Method: EXAONE Finance采用无注意力架构，用两种线性时间算子替代自注意力：1）因果1D卷积负责时间维度的混合；2）分组感知池化多层感知器（MLP）负责变量维度的混合。此外，通过掩码上下文增强（masked context augmentation）在训练期间引入连续的缺失区间，提高模型对缺失数据的鲁棒性。模型在大规模金融语料上预训练，覆盖股票、外汇、大宗商品、加密资产、固定收益和宏观经济指标等多类资产。

Result: 在覆盖多种资产类别的金融预测基准FinVerse上，EXAONE Finance取得了最先进的性能，在全部三个评估层级——点预测精度、截面资产排名和组合盈利性——均排名第一。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过设计专用的无注意力架构并针对金融数据特点（长序列、多变量、缺失普遍）进行预训练，可以构建比通用时间序列基础模型更适合金融预测的专业模型。EXAONE Finance在多个评估维度上验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: This technical report presents EXAONE Forecast for Finance (EXAONE Finance), a financial time series (TS) foundation model (TSFM) tailored to financial forecasting. Recent TSFMs achieve strong zero-shot performance through large-scale pretraining. However, they are primarily developed for general-domain TS and largely rely on self-attention backbones whose computational cost grows quadratically with sequence length and variate count. Moreover, they assume fully observed inputs and are pretrained on corpora that fail to capture the unique dynamics of financial markets. These limitations hinder their applicability to finance, where long, many-channel, intermittently observed panels are common. To address these challenges, EXAONE Finance adopts an attention-free architecture, replacing self-attention with two simple yet effective linear-time operators: 1) a causal 1D convolution for temporal mixing and 2) a group-aware pooling multi-layer perceptron (MLP) for variate mixing. Furthermore, a masked context augmentation exposes the model to contiguous missing spans during training, improving robustness to the missingness pervasive in financial markets. EXAONE Finance is pretrained on a large-scale financial corpus covering not only equities but also foreign exchange, commodities, crypto-assets, fixed income, and macroeconomic indicators. On FinVerse, a financial forecasting benchmark covering diverse asset classes, EXAONE Finance attains state-of-the-art performance, ranking first across all three evaluation tiers---point-forecast accuracy, cross-sectional asset ranking, and portfolio profitability.

</details>


### [122] [From Matching Models to Recruiting Agents: A Systematized Narrative Review of AI Recruitment Systems, Evaluation, and Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04286)
*Ziyi Zhao,Guanzheng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统化梳理了人工智能招聘从"双边检索与排序"向"多阶段工作流（证据检索、候选比较、动作执行）"演进的历程，覆盖40篇代表性文献，识别了三个核心转变（相似性→双向适配性、单一模型→复合工作流、离线预测→证据与生产力对齐的评估），并指出了评估中暴露的系统性缺口与未来研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 招聘领域的人工智能自动化对象已从简历对与榜单转向多阶段工作流，但现有文献对这一演变缺乏系统梳理；同时，最终的评估实践（如产出分数）掩盖了管道故障，行为标签混淆了曝光、偏好与资格，私有/合成数据限制外部效度，隐私与安全未被联合评估，亟需一次整合性的综述以厘清进展并形成评估主张的证据映射。

Method: 采用"系统化叙事综述"方法，运用目的性检索与编码协议（更新至2026年7月23日），并辅以截至2026年9月2日的定向更新；整理了40篇代表性工作及配套的工业与法律文献；在领域、配对、列表、案例、轨迹与结果六个证据层级上对文档理解、检索、排序、评估、面试、寻源与人机交接环节进行分析。

Result: 识别出三个耦合转变（相似性→双向适配性、模型→复合工作流、离线预测→证据/生产力导向评估）；区分了六种证据层级；发现显著缺口：行为标签混淆曝光与偏好、私有/合成数据限制外部效度、最终输出分数掩盖管道失败、编码集内未直接评估隐私、且没有一行同时评估效用、公平、隐私与安全四维指标。

Conclusion: 提出从评估证据到最强可辩护主张的分阶段映射，并给出构建"双向、基于证据、时间受控、选择性、可审计"系统的研究议程；判断进展的标准是：工作流能否检索正确证据、保留不确定性、支持可争议决策，并在明确的成本与风险约束下改善结果。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence in recruitment has shifted the object being automated from profile pairs and ranked lists to multi-stage workflows that retrieve evidence, compare candidates, and support or execute actions. This systematized narrative review traces that development from bilateral retrieval and behavioral ranking through neural person--job matching, large language model (LLM) components, and tool-using recruiting agents. Using a purposive search and coding protocol updated through 23 July 2026, plus targeted updates through 2 September 2026, we organize 40 representative works with supporting industrial and legal sources. This synthesis is not a prevalence estimate. We analyze three coupled transitions: from similarity to reciprocal suitability, from a model to a compound workflow, and from offline prediction to evidence- and productivity-aligned evaluation. Across document understanding, retrieval, ranking, assessment, interviewing, sourcing, and human handoff, we distinguish field-, pair-, list-, case-, trajectory-, and outcome-level evidence. Persistent gaps arise because behavioral labels confound exposure, preference, and qualification; private and synthetic data limit external validity; final-output scores conceal pipeline failures; and, within the coded set, privacy is not directly evaluated and no row jointly evaluates utility, fairness, privacy, and security. These observations describe the coded set rather than the field as a whole. We therefore introduce a staged mapping from evaluation evidence to the strongest defensible claim, together with an agenda for reciprocal, evidence-grounded, temporally controlled, selective, and auditable systems. Progress should be judged by whether workflows retrieve the right evidence, preserve uncertainty, support contestable decisions, and improve outcomes under explicit cost and risk constraints.

</details>


### [123] [Harbor Adapters and Harbor-Index: Infrastructure and a Curated Meta-Dataset for Large-Scale Agentic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04298)
*Lin Shi,Haowei Lin,Zixuan Zhu,Xiaoyue Zhou,Xiang Li,Xiangning Lin,Yaxuan Deng,Han Xu,Yuangang Li,Shanda Li,Zizhao Chen,Hanwen Xing,Harsh Raj,Bo Chen,Quan Shi,Steven Dillmann,Yipeng Gao,Puneesh Khanna,Ruofan Lu,Chao Beyond Zhou,Michael Yang,Robert Zhang,Siyuan Chai,Jiayu Chang,Yizhao Chen,Xiaokun Chen,Yiwei Dai,Wenting Yang,Hange Liu,Minghao Liu,Zihan Wang,Adnan El Assadi,Benedikt Stroebl,E. Kelly Buchanan,Han Meng,Junwei He,Longxuan Yu,Radin Shayanfar,Yukyung Lee,Zhikang Dong,Allen G Hart,Anjiang Wei,Anurag Kashyap,Arpandeep Khatua,Audrey Jixin Zheng,Chengrui Ma,David Heineman,Dubing Chen,Hai-Anh Trinh,Haishuo Fang,Hefan Zhang,Hui Shen,Issa Sugiura,Jiankai Sun,Jiechao Gao,Junhong Lin,Junnan Li,Kai Yang,Lei Hsiung,Maoyu Wang,Mengze Tang,Nabil Omi,Negin Raoof,Nicholas Edwards,Octavia Guo,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Pengliang Ji,Przemysław Hejman,Qi Qi,Qunshu Lin,Richard Zhuang,Rui Yang,Ruichen Zheng,Ryan Marten,Shaghayegh Fazliani,Shizheng Hou,Sicong Jiang,Sijie Li,Song Bian,Terry Yue Zhuo,Tianqing Wu,Tom Tang,Wanjia Zhao,Weihao Xuan,Wenhua Liang,Xian Liu,Xin Lan,Xuan Zhang,Xuandong Zhao,Yanchuan Tang,Yifan Jiang,Yijiang Li,Yitong Guan,Yizhi Li,Yonghui Liu,Yuheng Tang,Yujun,Mao,Yunfei Zhao,Yuxin Wang,Yuxuan Tang,Zhenheng Tang,Zhifei Li,Ziruo Wang,Ziyu She,Kaiyuan Liu,Iheb Chaabane,Yuxin Tang,Xiangyi Li,Andy Konwinski,Boxuan Li,Leon Liangyu Chen,Alex Dimakis,Nicholas Carlini,Soroush Vosoughi,Di He,Etash Guha,Benjamin Feuer,Mike Merrill,Ludwig Schmidt,Alex Shaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Harbor Adapters，一个统一的智能体基准评测基础设施，将80多个基准转化为可评测任意智能体的适配器，引入Harbor-Index（82个精选高难度任务），并大规模评测了8个模型跨54个基准的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体基准数量的迅速增长，评估智能体变得越来越困难，因为这些基准通常需要复杂的环境和智能体集成。缺乏统一的评测基础设施导致评测成本高、可比性差，难以在大规模范围内进行可靠性强的智能体能力评估。

Method: (1) 开发基准适配器，将80多个基准移植以评测任意智能体，并通过严格的代码审查与一致性实验进行验证；(2) 在54个基准上对8个模型（覆盖不同能力层级）进行大规模评测，每个模型均使用Terminus-2及3种原生框架之一运行；(3) 通过难度过滤、AI与人工审计以及审计-修复循环，从适配后的基准套件中提炼出Harbor-Index——涵盖29个基准的82个多样化高质量高难度任务。

Result: Harbor-Index在大规模智能体评测的挑战性与覆盖广度上得以保持，同时测试成本可控。测试的所有模型-框架配置均未超过30%的通过率，最强的配置（GPT-5.5搭配Codex）达到28.0%。

Conclusion: 研究团队以开源形式发布了适配器、评测结果、深入分析以及Harbor-Index，以支持对语言模型智能体进行更可靠和全面的评测。

Abstract: Evaluating agents on the growing number of agentic benchmarks is challenging because they often require complex environments and agent integrations. We introduce Harbor Adapters, a unified evaluation infrastructure for agentic benchmarks. Our work makes three contributions. First, we develop benchmark adapters that port more than 80 benchmarks to evaluate arbitrary agents, and validate them through rigorous code review and parity experiments. Second, we conduct a large-scale evaluation of 8 models spanning capability tiers across 54 benchmarks; every model is run with Terminus-2 and with one of 3 native harnesses. This enables a broader analysis of agent capabilities and failure modes than was previously possible. Third, we introduce Harbor-Index, a curated set of 82 difficult, diverse, and high-quality tasks spanning 29 benchmarks, refined from the adapted suite through difficulty filtering, AI and human audit, and an audit-and-fix loop. Harbor-Index preserves the challenge and breadth of large-scale agentic evaluations while being affordable to run; no evaluated model-harness configuration exceeds 30% pass rate, and the strongest (GPT-5.5 with Codex) reaches 28.0%. We release the adapters, evaluation results, in-depth analysis, and Harbor-Index as open-source artifacts to support more reliable and comprehensive evaluation of language-model agents.

</details>


### [124] [Data-Optimized Contingency Screening: A Machine Learning Approach to Power System Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04300)
*Joshua Salako,Folajimi Osikomaiya,Olakorede Olamiju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨使用机器学习算法（KNN、RF、SVM）对电力系统暂态事故进行安全等级分类（安全/中等/严重），结合SMOTE过采样和PCA降维处理数据，在IEEE-14和IEEE-30节点系统上进行N-k故障分析。实验表明RF表现最优（F1分数高达0.97），PCA对整体性能贡献大于SMOTE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力系统安全至关重要，有效的故障分类能够支持主动决策并缓解大规模电力崩溃。传统事故分析方法可能不足以满足实时安全评估需求，因此需要探索更具可扩展性和高效性的机器学习方法。

Method: 使用牛顿-拉夫逊潮流法从N-k故障场景（k=1,2,3）中提取IEEE-14和IEEE-30母线系统数据，以总体性能指数（OPI）作为安全性指标。数据预处理结合SMOTE（解决类别不平衡）和PCA（降维），并在四种混合预处理配置（归一化、SMOTE平衡、PCA变换、SMOTE+PCA组合）下训练KNN、RF和SVM分类器，采用精确率、召回率和F1分数评估，重点关注严重故障类别。

Result: RF在IEEE-30和IEEE-14系统上取得最高F1分数（分别为0.97和0.86）；SVM显著受益于PCA从而提高分类准确率；KNN最适合SMOTE+PCA变换组合。研究发现PCA对模型整体性能的贡献大于SMOTE，SMOTE虽能提升召回率但会引入误报以牺牲精确率。

Conclusion: 机器学习可作为传统电力系统事故分析的可扩展且高效的替代方案，提升实时安全评估能力。在预处理策略中，PCA比SMOTE更具价值，实际应用中需根据需求权衡精确率与召回率之间的取舍。

Abstract: Ensuring the security of the power system is essential for stability and reliability, especially in the event of disruption. Effective classification of contingency in power systems enables proactive decision-making and mitigates large-scale breakdowns and failures. This study explores the use of machine learning algorithms to classify security levels of contingencies in power systems into safe, moderate or severe classes. For this approach, Newton-Raphson load flow method extracts system data from contingency scenarios, using Overall Performance Index (OPI) as safety measure. For data pre-processing, Synthetic Minority Over-Sampling Technique (SMOTE) and Principal Component Analysis (PCA) is used to address class imbalance and reduce dimensionality, respectively. K-Nearest Neighbours (KNN), Random Forest (RF) and Support Vector Machines (SVM) is trained and evaluated on datasets generated through N-k contingency scenarios for k equal 1, 2, and 3 on IEEE-14 and IEEE-30 bus systems using four hybrid pre-processing configurations: normalized, SMOTE-balanced, PCA-transformed, and a combined SMOTE PCA-transformed. Performance is assessed by precision, recall and F1 score, with priority given to the severe contingency classes. The RF achieved the highest F1 scores of 0.97 in IEEE-30 and 0.86 in IEEE-14, SVM benefits significantly from PCA and improves the accuracy of the classification, while KNN is best suited for SMOTE and PCA conversion. The findings show that PCA contributes more than SMOTE to the overall performance of the model. However, SMOTE improves recall but can introduce false positives and is therefore a compromise of accuracy. This study highlights machine learning as a scalable and powerful alternative to traditional contingency analysis, which improves the assessment of security in real time.

</details>


### [125] [Iris: Climbing to the Search Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04304)
*Ziyuan Liu,Hengqi Liu,Zichuan Wang,Yang Qin,Jiachen Liang,Xu Chu,Shaowei Chen,Yuantao Gu,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Iris-mini（35B-A3B）和Iris-pro（397B-A17B）两个搜索智能体，并公布了完整的数据流水线和训练配方。核心方法包括：从网页超链接结构反向构建多跳问题链、将非答案实体改写为描述性引用以防止字符串匹配泄漏、以参考模型区分开卷/闭卷表现来筛选问题；在SFT和RL两阶段交替训练（称为SFT-RL climbing），并配有请求级中断恢复和推理期上下文管理。在BrowseComp、BrowseComp-ZH、DeepSearchQA和HLE上分别达到82.2/84.8/86.9/52.3和88.6/85.1/92.9/56.4的成绩，是各自参数规模下开源搜索智能体的最强结果。作者将发布模型权重及完整配方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索智能体（search agent）是连接大型语言模型与实时网络信息的关键能力，但现有开源搜索智能体在需要多跳推理和深度搜索的基准上表现有限。作者希望通过公开高质量的训练数据流水线和训练配方，在35B和397B两个规模上构建出具有SOTA性能的搜索智能体，缩小开源与闭源系统之间的差距，并验证可复现的训练方法论。

Method: 1）任务构造：从网页语料的超链接结构中反向构建多跳问题链（基于实体图），将非答案实体改写为描述性引用以防止字符串匹配捷径，并用参考模型闭卷失败但开卷成功的准则筛选问题。2）SFT阶段：将问题转为轨迹数据，在轨迹级和轮次级进行过滤后做监督微调。3）RL阶段：针对实时搜索进行策略优化，奖励裁判和观察摘要器在训练集群内提供服务，超长回滚在请求级中断并从已提交前缀恢复。4）SFT-RL climbing：交替执行SFT和RL两阶段，将每轮RL中最难解决且最高效的回滚回送到下一轮SFT。5）推理期上下文管理：在固定工具集、上下文限制和裁判的条件下，评估有无该管理的性能差异。所有结果来自单一ReAct智能体，不使用子代理或测试期验证。

Result: 在启用推理期上下文管理后，Iris-mini和Iris-pro在四个基准上的成绩分别为：BrowseComp 82.2/88.6、BrowseComp-ZH 84.8/85.1、DeepSearchQA 86.9/92.9、HLE 52.3/56.4。这是各自参数范围内开源搜索智能体的最强整体结果。研究还表明，推理期上下文管理在这些基准上的价值超过系统间的多数报告差异。

Conclusion: 作者计划公开模型权重以及包括数据构建、训练和评估在内的完整配方，以推动开源搜索智能体的可复现发展。结论表明，SFT-RL climbing交替训练策略、严格的任务构造与过滤、以及推理期上下文管理共同构成了高性能搜索智能体的关键要素。

Abstract: We present Iris-mini and Iris-pro, two search agents trained at the 35B-A3B and 397B-A17B scales, together with the data pipeline and training recipe behind them. Tasks are reverse-constructed from the hyperlink structure of a web corpus: we author multi-hop chains over an entity graph distilled from a seed page and its out-links, rewrite every non-answer entity into a descriptive reference so that no clue can be resolved by string matching, and admit only questions that a reference model fails closed-book yet solves once the supporting evidence is supplied. These questions are then turned into trajectories, which are filtered at both the trajectory and the turn level before SFT. The policy is then optimized by RL against live search, with the reward judge and the observation summarizer served inside the training cluster, and with over-long rollouts interrupted at the request level and resumed from their committed prefix at the next step. We alternate the two stages in a procedure we call SFT-RL climbing, returning the hardest solved and most efficient rollouts of each RL round to the next supervised pass. Because inference-time context management is worth more on these benchmarks than most reported differences between systems, we evaluate every benchmark both with and without it, holding the tool set, the context limit, and the judge fixed. All results come from a single ReAct agent, with no sub-agents and no test-time verification. With management enabled, on BrowseComp, BrowseComp-ZH, DeepSearchQA, and HLE the two models reach $82.2/84.8/86.9/52.3$ and $88.6/85.1/92.9/56.4$, the strongest overall results among open-source search agents in their respective parameter ranges. We plan to release the model weights together with the complete recipe for data construction, training, and evaluation.

</details>


### [126] [A Removal Based Approach to Improve LLM Faithfulness at Test-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04343)
*Qinglan Luo,S M A Nahian,John Guttag,S. Mazdak Abulnaga,Katie Matton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种测试时（test-time）方法直接应对LLM解释不忠实的"不完整性"问题：将解释中未提及的概念从输入中移除并重新查询模型，从而消除隐藏影响，提升解释的忠实度。该方法无需修改模型参数，在两个数据集、多个模型家族和两个独立忠实度指标上均优于标准提示和鼓励忠实的提示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被越来越多地用于高利害决策，其解释是审计模型行为的重要工具，但这些解释可能不忠实——未能反映模型决策背后的真实推理。现有解释不忠实包含两个维度：不完整性（遗漏影响答案的因素）和不可靠性/不健全性（引用未影响答案的因素）。训练时方法需要访问模型权重和大量计算资源，而测试时方法主要聚焦于解决不健全性，缺乏直接针对不完整性的方案。

Method: 该方法是一个测试时（推理时）的算法：给定一个问题及模型生成的答案和解释，首先识别出解释中未提及的概念（即未被归因的概念），然后将这些概念从原始输入中移除，得到缩减后的输入，再重新查询模型。这样在保留已提及概念影响力的同时，消除了未提及概念对答案的潜在影响。通过对比原始回答与缩减输入下的回答来评估和改善解释的完整性。该方法与模型无关，不需要修改模型参数。

Result: 在两个数据集、多个模型家族以及两个独立的忠实度评估指标上，该方法相比标准提示和鼓励忠实度的提示方法均提升了解释的忠实度。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种灵活的机制来减少隐藏影响，提升LLM辅助决策的可靠性和安全性，可在推理时直接应用而不需要修改模型参数，从而补充现有主要针对不健全性的测试时方法，直接应对解释不完整性问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for consequential decisions, making their explanations an important tool for auditing model behavior. Unfortunately, these explanations can be unfaithful, failing to reflect the actual reasoning underlying the model's decisions. We consider a setting in which an LLM provides both an answer and an explanation in response to a question. We identify two distinct dimensions of unfaithful explanations: incompleteness, meaning that the explanation omits factors that influence the answer, and unsoundness, meaning that the explanation cites factors that did not influence the model's answer. Existing approaches to improving LLM faithfulness include training-time methods, which require access to model weights and extensive computational resources, and test-time methods that largely focus on addressing unsoundness. We introduce a test-time approach that directly targets incompleteness. We remove from the input the concepts not credited in the model's explanation and re-query the model on the reduced input. This eliminates unmentioned influences while preserving the influence of mentioned concepts. Across two datasets, multiple model families, and two independent faithfulness metrics, our approach improves explanation faithfulness compared to both standard prompting and prompting to encourage faithfulness. Our method is model-agnostic and can be applied at inference time without modifying model parameters, providing a flexible mechanism for reducing hidden influences and improving the reliability and safety of LLM-assisted decision making.

</details>


### [127] [Why Better Models Can Create Riskier Systems: Evidence from LLM Agents in Financial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04373)
*Jillian Ross,Eric So,Zoe De Simone,Charles Pozniak,Andrew W. Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了"能力悖论"：提升大语言模型的个体能力反而可能降低系统层面的表现。研究发现，由于共享的训练数据和架构，更强大的LLM会表现出更相似的行为，产生无法通过多样化消除的相关性风险。通过在金融市场中使用不同能力的LLM交易者进行基于智能体的模拟，作者发现前沿LLM的行为相关性随能力增强而增加，在准确推理时提高参与度可降低市场风险，但在共享错误信息环境下，相关行为反而成为负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正被大规模部署于金融、内容审核、招聘等关键现实系统中，但现有研究主要关注个体模型能力的提升，缺乏对系统层面（多个同构模型协同运行）结果的考量。作者希望揭示个体能力与系统结果之间的非直觉关系。

Method: 作者构建了一个一般性框架来说明模型间相关性如何产生不可分散的风险下限（risk floor），然后在金融市场中使用不同通用能力的LLM交易者进行基于智能体的模拟来检验框架预测。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）前沿LLM表现出显著相关的行为，且相关性随能力增强而上升；（2）当共享推理准确时，增加智能体参与能降低市场风险；（3）当智能体共享错误信息环境时，同样的相关行为变成负债。

Conclusion: 结果识别出一个能力悖论：提升个体模型的能力并不必然带来更好的系统级结果。关于相同动态是否在其他领域出现，仍需开放的经验研究来验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are being deployed at scale in consequential real-world systems, from financial markets to content moderation to hiring. We show that improving individual model capability can degrade rather than improve system-level outcomes. We hypothesize that shared training and architectures can lead more capable LLMs to behave more similarly, creating correlated actions that do not diversify away. We develop a general framework showing how this correlation creates a non-diversifiable risk floor and test its predictions in financial markets using an agent-based simulation with LLM traders of varying general-purpose capability. We find that: (1) frontier LLMs exhibit significantly correlated behavior that increases with capability; (2) when their shared reasoning is accurate, increasing agent participation reduces market-level risk; and (3) when agents share a common misinformation environment, the same correlated behavior becomes a liability. Together, these results identify a capability paradox: improving individual models does not necessarily produce better system-level outcomes. Whether the same dynamics arise in other domains is an open empirical question.

</details>


### [128] [Corporate Language Model (CLM): Transforming Tacit and Fragmented Enterprise Knowledge into a Sovereign, Auditable, and Executable Corporate Intelligence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04377)
*Fabricio C. Avini,Guilherme Trez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出企业语言模型（CLM）框架，将企业的结构化、非结构化、多模态及隐性知识转化为基于本体论的企业基础，支持推理与受治理的执行，以解决企业AI部署因缺乏组织结构化底层而失败的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI部署失败的根本原因并非模型能力不足，而是组织缺乏编码其决策、协商与执行方式的结构化底层。通用LLM不携带企业特定的本体论先验；RAG易碎且无法通向可执行动作；静态剧本虽编码逻辑但无法推理或适应。因此需要将隐性知识捕获、本体论扎根、主权部署与可审计执行作为从一开始就协同设计的架构。

Method: CLM框架包含五个能力平面和四个架构支柱：神经符号网格（Neurosymbolic Mesh）将生成模型与知识图谱耦合；技能图（Skill Graph）对可复用的战术、角色画像、异议与目标进行类型化与组合；活体数字孪生（Living Digital Twins）将功能领域建模为推理代理；深度安全层（Deep Security Layer）强制主权、可追溯性与人类监督。此外采用Spec-as-Code范式桥接扎根意图与可执行制品。

Result: 论文提出四项贡献：将CLM定义为独立研究对象；引入技能图实现按构造可组合的可解释性；提出"智慧倾听者"效应，即具备隐性知识能力的基础随使用而复合增值，与动态能力及组织学习相关；来自巴西一家JCI认证的三级医院的证据实例化了LGPD合规下的六个成熟度阶段中的三个。

Conclusion: CLM是这一以基础为中心的架构类别的一种实例化，其核心主张是企业级AI基础应同时具备隐性知识捕获、本体论扎根、主权部署与可审计执行能力，并通过实证展示了该框架在真实医疗机构中的初步落地可行性。

Abstract: Enterprise AI deployments fail not from model inadequacy, but because organizations lack a structured substrate encoding how they decide, negotiate, and execute. Generic LLMs carry no firm-specific ontological priors; RAG remains brittle, with no path to executable action; static playbooks encode logic but cannot reason or adapt. This demands an architecture treating tacit-knowledge capture, ontological grounding, sovereign deployment, and auditable actuation as co-designed from the start. This paper introduces the Corporate Language Model (CLM), a framework transforming a firm's structured, unstructured, multimodal, and tacit knowledge into an ontology-grounded enterprise foundation upon which reasoning and governed execution are composed. CLM has five capability planes and four architectural pillars: a Neurosymbolic Mesh coupling generative models with a knowledge graph; a Skill Graph where reusable tactics, personas, objections, and goals are typed and composed; Living Digital Twins modeling functional areas as reasoning surrogates; and a Deep Security Layer enforcing sovereignty, traceability, and human oversight. A Spec-as-Code paradigm bridges grounded intent and executable artifact. CLM is one instantiation of this foundation-centric class. Four contributions follow: CLM is defined as a distinct object of study; the Skill Graph is introduced for compositional explainability by construction; the Wisdom Listener effect is proposed, whereby tacit-capable foundations compound in value with use, connecting to dynamic capabilities and organizational learning; and evidence from a JCI-accredited tertiary hospital in Brazil instantiates three of the six maturity stages under LGPD.

</details>


### [129] [BioSync: Transformer-Based Cross-Modal Fusion for a Multimodal Physiological Digital Biomarker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04504)
*Seyed Mahmoud Sajjadi Mohammadabadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioSync提出了一种结合多模态穿戴设备数据（心率变异性、脑电、活动记录、语音等）的复合数字生物标志物BSI，使用多头自注意力与线性分支的宽深架构，在两个合成队列上表现略优于简单的特征拼接方法，但在真实队列上的验证仍需开展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一模态的可穿戴/移动设备测量数据对生理状态仅有部分且含噪声的观察视角；多个模态联合观察可能包含任何单一模态都不具备的信息，因此需要一种能够有效整合多模态生理信号的复合数字生物标志物模型。

Method: BioSync采用多头自注意力处理模态token，并增加一个假设类包含标准特征拼接的线性分支，形成宽深（wide-and-deep）结合的架构。该设计受潜变量测量理论启发，在BEST框架下定义连续的复合数字生物标志物BSI。在两个文献驱动的合成队列（四模态认知衰退队列和基于AI-READI可穿戴模式的代谢-自主神经队列）上评估，并与单模态、早期融合、晚期融合及纯注意力消融等设计进行对比。

Result: 认知队列中BioSync和特征拼接AUC分别为0.928和0.926；代谢队列中BioSync准确率/F1为0.764/0.766，优于拼接的0.756/0.758。BSI与两个队列的潜在严重程度相关（r=0.91和0.68）。纯注意力消融AUC为0.911，表明性能提升来自宽深架构。在匹配模态dropout训练下，BioSync在5/6的认知队列损坏率和最高的代谢队列损坏率下优于拼接。其认知队列AUC高于五个已发表的数字生物标志物参考值。

Conclusion: BioSync的多模态融合架构在合成队列上显示出优于传统特征拼接和纯注意力方案的潜力，但仅与拼接基线存在微小差距，且数据集与任务差异不支持权威对比声明；在真实临床队列上的验证仍属必需。

Abstract: Cardiac, neural, behavioral, and speech measurements from wearable and mobile devices provide partial, noise-sensitive views of physiological state. BioSync combines these measurements into the \textbf{BioSync Index (BSI)}, a continuous composite digital biomarker defined under the BEST framework. The model applies multi-head self-attention to modality tokens and adds a linear branch whose hypothesis class includes standard feature concatenation. This architecture is motivated by latent-variable measurement theory and by the possibility that joint observations contain information unavailable from individual modalities. We evaluated BioSync on two literature-informed synthetic cohorts: a four-modality cognitive-decline cohort using HRV, EEG, actigraphy, and speech, and a metabolic-autonomic cohort structured around the public AI-READI wearable schema. In the cognitive cohort, BioSync and concatenation obtained AUCs of 0.928 and 0.926, respectively. In the metabolic cohort, BioSync obtained accuracy/F1 of 0.764/0.766, compared with 0.756/0.758 for concatenation. The BSI correlated with latent severity in both cohorts ($r=0.91$ and $r=0.68$). A pure-attention ablation obtained cognitive-cohort AUC 0.911, locating the increase to 0.928 in the combined wide-and-deep architecture. With matched modality-dropout training, BioSync led concatenation at five of six cognitive-cohort corruption rates and at the highest metabolic-cohort rate. Its cognitive-cohort AUC was also higher than five published digital-biomarker reference values, although differences in datasets and tasks preclude a controlled benchmark claim. Comparison with single-modality, early-fusion, and late-fusion designs across six prespecified criteria identifies the model's computational properties; validation on real cohorts remains necessary.

</details>


### [130] [HarvestBench: Measuring Whether LLM Agents Will Pay to Avoid Killing Animals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04444)
*Jasmine Brazilek,Miles Tidmarsh,Matthias Endres,Anshuman Singh,Jeremiah Miller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HarvestBench是首个为"避免副作用"定价且将副作用明确命名为生物体的基准测试。它基于强化学习网格世界的农场模拟，让LLM子代理操控两台拖拉机在田中有动物的环境下协作收割玉米。实验对9个模型进行了7,201次定价决策，杀死率从0.4%到98.8%不等，且与模型能力无关。模型对价格敏感度不同（弹性0.09至1.69），伦理提示对减少杀生有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的副作用基准测试衡量的是代理在达成目标过程中对环境的副作用，但没有一个给"避免副作用"贴上实际代价标签，也没有把副作用的受害者明确定义为有生命的生物。作者希望衡量模型在面临真实代价（燃料成本）时，是否愿意为避免伤害生物而付出代价，而非仅仅询问模型对伤害的看法。

Method: 构建农场模拟环境（强化学习网格世界）：两个LLM子代理驱动两台拖拉机进行集体玉米收割，田地中有动物。拖拉机被动物阻挡时，自动导航停止并向模型询问是否径直碾压（无燃料成本）还是绕行（需支付公布的燃料价格）。对照组为岩石（会损坏拖拉机）和干草捆（无害且非生物）。还设计了从邻居田地偷取作物的选项作为第二项道德测试。所有决策均在无记忆条件下进行，伤害从未在目标中被明确指出。评分器直接统计游戏日志中的事件，不依赖LLM评判。

Result: 9个模型共产生7,201次定价决策，其中3,951次涉及动物。杀生率从0.4%（Terra和Sol最仁慈）到98.8%（GPT-4o-mini最残忍），且与模型能力无相关性。6个模型中有4个对价格显著敏感（5%显著性水平），价格弹性0.09至1.69。所有9个模型在默认地图中对野生动物比饲养动物的碾压率更高。道德提示的影响最大：在道德提示下，6个推理模型中有5个的杀生率低于6%；移除提示后所有6个模型杀生率均超过84%。

Conclusion: HarvestBench提供了一个完全可复现、无需LLM评判的基准测试，能够衡量模型为避免伤害所愿意付出的实际代价，而非其口头承诺。研究揭示了不同模型在"生命vs成本"权衡上的巨大行为差异，且这些差异与模型能力排序无关。模型的行为对情境提示高度敏感，说明当前LLM在伦理决策上缺乏稳定性，这为评估和改进LLM的伦理行为提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Benchmarks for the side effects an agent causes on the way to a goal already exist, but HarvestBench is the first to put a price on avoiding the side effect and to name that side effect as a living creature. It is a farm simulation: LLM sub-agents drive a crew of two tractors through a cooperative corn harvest, with animals in the field. The environment is a reinforcement learning gridworld, every decision is made without memory, and the harm is never named in the goal. When an animal blocks a tractor's route the autopilot stops and asks the model whether to drive on, at no fuel cost, or swerve around it for a posted fuel price. Kills are compared against two controls: rocks, which damage the tractor and are hit under 1% of the time by every model, and hay bales, which are harmless and not alive. Models can also take crops from the neighbor's field instead of their own, a second test of what they treat as moral.
  Across nine models and 7,201 priced decisions, 3,951 involved an animal rather than a hay bale or a rock. Kill rates range from 0.4% to 98.8%, with Terra and Sol the most merciful and GPT-4o-mini the most cruel, and they are not ordered by capability. Four of six models were sensitive to price at the 5% level, with elasticities from 0.09 to 1.69. All nine drove over wild animals more often than farmed animals on the default map, and the direction held at every map geometry in every model with room to move. The briefing mattered most: under the morality briefing the kill rate was under 6% in five of six reasoning models, and removing it raised the kill rate above 84% in all six.
  HarvestBench uses no LLM grader. The scorer counts events in the game log, so it is fully reproducible, and it measures what a model will pay to avoid harm rather than what it says about harm.

</details>


### [131] [A Tree-based RAG Framework for Evidence-Intensive QA via Adaptive Planning and Topology-Aware Evidence Gathering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04981)
*Songeun Lee,Kyungjin Min,Injae Na,Suyeong Lee,Chiyoung Kim,Woohwan Jung*

Main category: cs.AI
